### [1] [A new approach to the reaction-diffusion systems modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13636)
*Maxim Nazarov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于常微分方程组（ODE）的反应-扩散系统建模新方法，该方法在模型层面作为偏微分方程（PDE）的纯替代方案，描述上类似于有限体积法（FVM），但采用统计简化和几何概率原理描述扩散。该方法成功实现了对反应-扩散系统进行经典动力系统理论的定性分析目标，但在数值计算效率提升方面仅部分达成，仅在特定的初始分子分布和扩散系数条件下有显著效率优势。文章还分别评估了新方法的建模误差以确定实际应用标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的反应-扩散系统建模依赖偏微分方程，需要专门的数值方法（如直线法）求解，其定性分析和数值实现方面存在局限。作者旨在提出一种新的建模范式，通过常微分方程组替代偏微分方程，以简化定性分析（借助经典动力系统理论）并提升数值实现效率。

Method: 新方法在模型层面替代偏微分方程，通过常微分方程组描述反应-扩散系统。该方法与有限体积法在描述上高度相似，但区别在于采用统计简化和几何概率原理来描述扩散过程，从而将空间离散化与扩散机制通过概率方式耦合。

Result: 定性分析目标成功实现：基于ODE系统可应用经典动力系统理论进行动力学定性分析。但数值效率目标仅部分实现：只有对于简单的初始分子分布和特定的扩散系数，才能在保证可接受精度的前提下获得显著的效率提升。文章还单独评估了该方法的建模误差，并以此制定实际适用性判据。

Conclusion: 该新方法论作为PDE模型层面的纯替代方案具有可行性，尤其在定性分析方面优势明显，但数值效率提升受限于特定条件，需通过建模误差评估确定其实际适用范围。

Abstract: We consider a new methodology for modelling the reaction-diffusion systems based on systems of ordinary differential equations. In contrary to the specialised numerical methods like straight line method, this new methodology is positioned as a pure alternative at the model level for partial differential equations. In its description, the new method is largely similar to the finite volume method, but unlike the latter, it uses statistical simplifications and principles of geometric probability to describe diffusion. The main objectives of this approach are to simplify the qualitative analysis of reaction-diffusion systems and to improve the efficiency of numerical model implementation. The first objective is successfully addressed, as it becomes possible to use the apparatus of classical dynamical systems theory for a qualitative analysis of model dynamics based on the systems of ordinary differential equations. The second objective is only partially addressed, as the gain in efficiency while maintaining acceptable accuracy for numerical implementation will be significant only for certain simple initial distribution of molecules and for specific diffusion coefficients. Furthermore, to formulate criteria for practical applicability, we separately evaluate the modelling error using this new methodology.

</details>


### [2] [Coupling-Aware Vanka Smoothing for Multigrid Preconditioning of the Implicit Immersed Boundary Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14310)
*Cole Gruninger,Boyce E. Griffith*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种耦合感知Vanka (CAV)平滑策略，用于浸没边界(IB)方法中隐式时间离散所产生的大型耦合线性系统的有效多重网格预条件处理。通过代数消去拉格朗日自由度获得约化的欧拉速度-压力IB系统，并利用欧拉弹性矩阵的图结构构建CAV补丁块。数值实验表明CAV预条件的FGMRES在9-15次迭代内将相对残差降低十个数量级，且网格细化时迭代次数增长极小，是首个针对时间相关隐式IB公式的鲁棒多重网格策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浸没边界(IB)方法将结构力学与流体动力学的拉格朗日和欧拉公式自然结合，但显式时间离散化IB力会对时间步长施加依赖于结构刚度的上限。隐式处理这些力可消除此限制，但随结构刚度增加，产生的耦合线性系统求解越来越困难。

Method: 通过代数消去拉格朗日自由度，得到约化的欧拉速度-压力IB系统。提出耦合感知Vanka (CAV)平滑策略：CAV补丁由标准以压力为中心的Vanka补丁的并集构成，由欧拉弹性矩阵的图结构决定哪些补丁被组合。CAV补丁大小在网格细化下有界，因此每循环多重网格的计算成本随欧拉自由度数线性增长。

Result: 使用靶点力、膜力和梁力定律的测试表明，CAV预条件的FGMRES在9-15次迭代内将相对残差降低十个数量级，网格细化下迭代数增长极小。在柔性纤维流动的非线性基准中，欧拉网格从32×32细化到256×256时，每次牛顿迭代的平均FGMRES迭代数仅从8.6增加到9.5。

Conclusion: CAV提供了首个针对时间相关隐式IB公式的鲁棒多重网格策略，具有网格无关的收敛性和线性计算复杂度，为高刚度流固耦合问题的高效求解开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The immersed boundary (IB) method models fluid--structure interaction using the natural Lagrangian and Eulerian formulations of structural mechanics and fluid dynamics, respectively, but explicit time discretization of the IB force imposes a stiffness-dependent upper bound on the time-step size. Treating these forces implicitly removes this restriction, but the resulting coupled linear systems become increasingly difficult to solve as the structural stiffness increases. Algebraically eliminating the Lagrangian degrees of freedom yields a reduced Eulerian velocity--pressure IB system. Here we introduce a coupling-aware Vanka (CAV) smoothing strategy to enable effective multigrid preconditioning of this system. CAV patches are built as unions of standard pressure-centered Vanka patches, with the graph of the Eulerian elasticity matrix determining which patches are combined. Under grid refinement, CAV patch sizes remain bounded, and the computational cost of each multigrid cycle thereby grows linearly with the number of Eulerian degrees of freedom. Tests using target-point, membrane, and beam force laws show that CAV-preconditioned FGMRES reduces the relative residual by ten orders of magnitude in $9--15$ iterations, with little growth under grid refinement. In a nonlinear benchmark modeling flow past a flexible fiber, the average number of FGMRES iterations per Newton solve increases only from $8.6$ to $9.5$ as the Eulerian grid is refined from $32\times32$ to $256\times256$ cells. To our knowledge, CAV provides the first robust multigrid strategy for time-dependent implicit IB formulations.

</details>


### [3] [An adaptive superconvergent mixed finite element method for exactly symmetric linear elasticity based on local residual minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13701)
*Ernesto Cáceres,Patrick Vega*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对线性弹性问题的精确对称混合有限元离散，提出了一种后验误差估计器。该方法将Stenberg型后处理方案重新表述为离散对偶范数下的局部残差最小化问题，从而以对偶变量的形式在零额外计算成本下获得局部残差的Riesz表示，并据此构建后验误差指示子。论文建立了可靠性估计（显式追踪Lamé参数依赖）和局部效率估计（无需辅助气泡函数），并提出了基于鲁棒稳定性和Oswald平均算子的替代可靠性估计，其常数在λ→∞时保持有界。同一指示子和同一比较范数同时适用于可压和不可压两种情形，与需要不同构造的超圆（hypercircle）类估计器形成对比。数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确对称混合有限元方法求解线性弹性问题时，需要可靠且高效的后验误差估计以实现自适应网格细化。现有超圆（hypercircle）类估计器在可压和不可压极限下需要不同的构造方案，且效率估计通常依赖辅助气泡函数。因此，需要一个统一的、对两种情形均适用的后验误差估计框架，同时保证可靠性常数在不可压极限下仍然有界。

Method: 将Stenberg型后处理方案重新表述为离散对偶范数下的局部残差最小化问题，以对偶变量形式获得局部残差的Riesz表示（零额外计算成本），由此构建后验误差指示子。理论分析方面：在标准可压情形下建立可靠性估计（显式追踪Lamé参数依赖）和无需气泡函数的局部效率估计；在不可压极限下，利用鲁棒稳定性性质和Oswald平均算子建立替代可靠性估计，使常数随λ→∞保持有界，并证明局部效率估计在两个情形下均匀有界。

Result: 建立了统一的后验误差估计框架：一个指示子和一个比较范数同时适用于可压和不可压两种情形；可靠性估计的常数在不可压极限下保持有界；局部效率估计无需辅助气泡函数且在所有情形下一致有界。数值实验（包括含已知奇异解的基准问题和无解析解的基准问题）验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为精确对称混合有限元提供了统一且鲁棒的后验误差估计器，克服了现有超圆类方法在可压/不可压情形需分别构造的缺陷，同时保证了两类估计（可靠性和效率）在极限情形下的最优性能。

Abstract: We introduce an a posteriori error estimator for exactly symmetric mixed finite element discretizations of linear elasticity, based on recasting a Stenberg-type postprocessing scheme as a local residual minimization problem in a discrete dual norm. This construction yields, as a dual variable and at no additional computational cost, a Riesz representative of the associated local residual, from which we build an a posteriori error indicator. We establish a reliability estimate, with the dependence on the Lamé parameters tracked explicitly, and a local efficiency estimate, without auxiliary bubble functions, in the standard compressible regime, as well as an alternative reliability estimate, based on a robust stability property and an Oswald averaging operator, with a constant that remains bounded as $λ\to\infty$; the local efficiency estimate holds, with the same bounded behavior, uniformly in both regimes. Notably, a single indicator and a single comparison norm serve both regimes, in contrast with existing hypercircle-based estimators, which require a distinct construction for the incompressible limit. Numerical examples, including a benchmark with a known singular solution and one without an analytical solution, validate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [4] [Approximating matrix functions by block Krylov methods with randomized vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13714)
*Josh Kane,Lucas Onisk,Lothar Reichel,Giuseppe Rodriguez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究如何高效近似计算形如 f(A)b 的表达式（其中A为大型方阵，f为函数，b为向量），提出使用随机化分块Krylov方法，即构建包含b和若干随机向量的初始块向量进行Krylov子空间逼近。计算实验表明，该方法相比标准Krylov方法可减少计算时间和Krylov迭代步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多应用数学领域中，需要计算 f(A)b 形式的表达式。当矩阵A规模很大时，直接计算 f(A) 不现实，通常做法是在由A和b张成的Krylov子空间中求近似值。然而标准Krylov方法在收敛速度上仍有提升空间，因此探索更高效的方法具有重要意义。

Method: 将随机化分块Krylov方法的多种变体应用于 f(A)b 的近似计算。核心思想是构造一个初始块向量，该块向量不仅包含原始向量b，还额外包含若干个随机生成的向量，从而在分块Krylov子空间中进行逼近。f只需要在一个小型矩阵上进行求值。

Result: 计算示例表明，采用包含b和若干随机向量的初始块向量的分块Krylov方法，相比标准Krylov方法能够在更少的Krylov迭代步数内达到同等精度，并且总体计算时间更短。

Conclusion: 随机化分块Krylov方法为大规模 f(A)b 计算提供了一种更高效的替代方案，通过引入随机初始块向量改善了收敛性能与计算效率。

Abstract: The need to evaluate expressions of the form $f(A)\mathbf{b}$, where $A$ is a square matrix, $f$ is a function, and $\mathbf{b}$ is a vector, arises in several areas of applied mathematics. When the matrix $A$ is very large, it is usually not attractive to evaluate $f(A)$. Instead, $f(A)\mathbf{b}$ often is approximated by computing an estimate in a Krylov subspace that depends on $A$ and $\mathbf{b}$, and only requires that $f$ be evaluated at a small matrix. This paper explores the application of several variants of randomized block Krylov methods to the approximation of $f(A)\mathbf{b}$. Computed examples suggest that block Krylov methods with an initial block vector that contains $\mathbf{b}$ as well as a few randomly generated vectors may require less computing time and reduce the number of Krylov steps than standard Krylov methods.

</details>


### [5] [An Efficient Solver for Finite Element-based Constrained Transport in 3D Magnetohydrodynamics Applied to Magnetic Confinement Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13829)
*Golo A. Wimmer,Konstantin Lipnikov,Ben S. Southworth,Xian-Zhu Tang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于电阻磁流体动力学（MHD）中刚性磁波耦合的高效求解器框架，基于隐-隐（IMIM）时间分裂方法，将磁波子系统转化为各向异性的curl-curl算子，利用辅助空间Maxwell（AMS）多重网格求解器，并采用curl-共形有限元空间保持离散磁散度约束，在三维托卡马克非线性测试案例中验证了求解器效率和结构保持离散格式的精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实托卡马克几何上的电阻MHD模拟中，刚性磁波耦合带来的数值刚度问题使得高效求解器难以构建，需要开发能够处理快速磁波与各向异性热输运的可扩展求解框架。

Method: 采用隐-隐（IMIM）时间分裂将快速磁波和各向异性热输运与较慢的声学动力学分离，同时保持全耦合；磁波子系统被构造为各向异性curl-curl算子，使其可利用可扩展的AMS多重网格求解器；离散层面使用curl-共形有限元空间表示磁场，并设计速度空间以保持Lorentz力耦合引入的curl-curl结构；这种兼容离散化保持了离散磁散度约束，并使线性系统可直接用于AMS求解器。

Result: 在完全非线性的三维托卡马克测试案例上，展示了求解器的效率以及结构保持离散格式的精度和稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的兼容离散化与AMS多重网格求解器结合的策略，能够为真实托卡马克几何上的电阻MHD问题提供高效、稳定且保持结构性质的数值求解方案。

Abstract: We present an efficient solver framework for the stiff magnetic wave coupling arising in resistive magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) on realistic tokamak geometries. The approach builds on an implicit-implicit (IMIM) time-splitting that separates fast magnetic waves and anisotropic heat transport from slower acoustic dynamics while retaining full coupling (Krzysik et al. 2026). Within this formulation, the magnetic wave subsystem appears as an anisotropic curl-curl operator, enabling the use of scalable auxiliary-space Maxwell (AMS) multigrid solvers. To exploit this structure at the discrete level, we employ curl-conforming finite element spaces for the magnetic field and design the velocity space to preserve the curl-curl structure induced by the Lorentz-force coupling. The resulting compatible discretization preserves the discrete magnetic divergence constraint while producing linear systems directly amenable to efficient AMS-based solvers. We demonstrate solver efficiency as well as the accuracy and stability of the resulting structure-preserving discretization on fully nonlinear three-dimensional tokamak test cases.

</details>


### [6] [Vanishing Clutter: Fast and Accurate Shape Imaging via Active Cloaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13839)
*Haoqiang Xiao,Guang-Hui Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对近场扫描光学显微镜（NSOM）中探针污染导致图像重建高度病态的问题，将主动隐形（active cloaking）从传统意义上的成像对抗机制转变为消除探针干扰的使能机制，通过最优控制问题与层状等离激元结构的局域反常共振理论推导出精确闭式解，实现近乎无探针的测量环境，从而大幅加速并提升亚波长形状重建的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NSOM虽然能够实现亚波长分辨率，但探针本身会扰动局域场，破坏信噪比并使定量形状重建的反问题高度病态。传统迭代重建方法直接处理被探针污染的数据，计算代价高昂且精度受限，亟需一种消除探针干扰、使反问题良态的预处理机制。

Method: 首先建立正向模型的适定性（well-posedness）作为理论基础；然后将主动隐形重新用作消除探针干扰的机制，通过构造最优控制问题并对探针进行主动隐形；利用层状等离激元结构中局域反常共振（localized anomalous resonance）理论，推导出最优控制问题的精确闭式解（closed-form minimizer），从而避免计算上昂贵的迭代求解；由此获得近乎无探针的测量环境，再据此进行快速无伪影的形状重建。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，该方法能够精确重建目标形状，且相比直接对探针污染数据应用传统迭代方法（无隐形预处理），加速通常达数量级（orders of magnitude），并验证了方法的鲁棒性，展现了其在高质量亚波长成像中的变革性潜力。

Conclusion: 主动隐形可以被反转用途，作为消除NSOM探针干扰的核心机制，配合闭式解的最优控制方法，能够以极低计算成本获得高保真、无伪影的亚波长形状重建，为超分辨成像提供了一种新的高效范式。

Abstract: Imaging a target sample in near-field scanning optical microscopy (NSOM) is fundamentally limited by measurement artifacts and data contamination from multiple probe-sample scattering. The probe, essential for subwavelength resolution, inherently perturbs the local field, degrading the signal-to-noise ratio and rendering the inverse problem for quantitative shape reconstruction highly ill-posed. We first establish the well-posedness of the corresponding forward model, providing a rigorous foundation for subsequent imaging. We then repurpose active cloaking--conventionally the antagonist of imaging--as an enabling mechanism to eliminate probe-induced interference. Rather than directly reconstructing the sample from corrupted data, we actively cloak the probe by formulating an optimal control problem and prove the existence and stability of its minimizers. Leveraging the theory of localized anomalous resonance in layered plasmonic structures, we derive an exact closed-form minimizer, thereby circumventing the computationally prohibitive iterative solution of the optimal control problem. The resulting cloaking-driven interference removal yields a virtually probe-free measurement environment, enabling fast, artifact-free shape reconstruction. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate accurate shape reconstruction and dramatic acceleration--often by orders of magnitude--over conventional iterative methods applied directly to probe--contaminated data without cloaking--based preprocessing, validating the robustness and transformative potential of the proposed approach for high-fidelity subwavelength imaging.

</details>


### [7] [PDE Realization and Structure-Aware Solvers for a Compact Two-Stage Fourth-Order IMEX Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13875)
*Zhixin Huo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向刚性分裂问题的四阶两阶段二导数隐式-显式时间离散的PDE实现与求解器框架。核心贡献在于：证明了二阶ADER/Cauchy-Kowalevski局部演化足以支撑四阶外层复合，建立了光滑重构一致性与全离散误差平衡，推导了分裂场自微分产生的Lie括号缺陷；通过非精确阶段分析给出保持四阶精度的非对称中点与端点残差容差；对加宽阶段采用无矩阵保留完整混合作用而仅在逆中近似主导刚性物理的求解策略，形成了二次与移位预条件子、Fourier与多层实现、半线性反应-扩散约简及源局部消除等求解器机制。数值消融实验验证了一致性与容差结果，二维Brusselator算例显示良好的误差-墙钟时间权衡，经典刚性前沿基准则揭示了空间激波-源分离局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种同时兼顾一致性与求解成本的PDE求解器框架。关键难点在于"一致性-成本耦合"：为保持时间积分器中显式-隐式混合相互作用需要全场时间微分，但该相互作用会加宽隐式阶段算子，使每次求解显著更昂贵。需要一个既保持四阶精度又能控制求解开销的完整方案。

Abstract: We conduct a convergence analysis for the Schrödinger Bridge Time Series (SBTS) data generator. Abstract: In the frequency domain, nonlinear acoustic and electromagnetic wave propagation can, in certain regimes, be modeled by second-harmonic generation systems consisting of two coupled nonlinear Helmholtz equations. We analyze a conforming finite element approximation of a scalar second-harmonic generation scattering problem truncated using an exact Dirichlet-to-Neumann boundary condition. The discretization uses continuous piecewise polynomial finite elements of degree $p$. Under small-data conditions involving the incident-field and nonlinear susceptibilities, we prove existence and uniqueness of the continuous and discrete nonlinear solutions in a prescribed small ball. In the same regime, we derive quasi-optimal a priori $H^1$-error estimates by a nonlinear Céa-type argument combining linear Galerkin quasi-optimality with small-data stability estimates for the coupled nonlinearities. We also analyze the convergence of the fixed-point iteration used to compute the discrete nonlinear solution and quantify the combined effects of finite element approximation and nonlinear iteration error. A manufactured-solution experiment illustrates the predicted finite element convergence rates, while additional PML-based scattering computations demonstrate the behavior of the nonlinear fixed-point solver in two and three dimensions.

Abstract: Solving parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) repeatedly for many parameter values,as needed in optimal control, uncertainty quantification, and inverse problems, is often prohibitive with classical discretizations. This chapter introduces Reduced Order Modeling (ROM), which builds compressed yet accurate representations of parametric solution sets for fast online evaluation. We formulate the approximation of parametric PDEs as a supervised learning task and review linear approximation, very effective for elliptic and parabolic problems, before turning to nonlinear methods needed when solutions exhibit discontinuities or steep gradients. We then show how to use reduced-order models to efficiently recover the state of a physical system from limited measurements, which is an inverse problem known as state estimation. Throughout, we favor results with theoretical guarantees while also discussing practical methods, and provide a companion Jupyter notebook implementing the main algorithms.

Abstract: We develop an arbitrary-degree geometry-conforming finite element (GC-FE) framework for two-dimensional elliptic boundary value and interface problems on curved domains. Using the Frenet--Serret transformation, curved-boundary and interface-fitted segments are represented exactly, while polynomials in Frenet coordinates generate generally nonpolynomial local shape functions in physical coordinates. For interface-unfitted meshes, GC-FE spaces on curved-boundary elements are coupled with geometry-conforming immersed finite element (GC-IFE) spaces on interface-cut elements, with standard polynomial spaces used elsewhere. We establish optimal approximation, inverse, and trace estimates for the GC-FE spaces. For fitted meshes, we prove well-posedness and optimal error estimates in energy and $L^2$ norms for a symmetric interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin discretization. By retaining the prescribed curves exactly, the method avoids the geometric variational crime associated with curved-geometry approximation and requires no corresponding geometric consistency estimates. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted rates, show global accuracy comparable to nodal isoparametric finite elements and smaller true-interface trace errors in the reported tests, and demonstrate the coupled GC-FE-GC-IFE method on interface-unfitted meshes.

Abstract: Hybrid dealiasing is an FFT-based method for computing linear convolutions of complex-valued data that reduces the cost of dealiasing by performing zero padding implicitly. We develop two new algorithms that extend hybrid dealiasing to real-valued convolutions.
  The first algorithm exploits conjugate symmetries in the transformed data and computes each residue contribution directly. The second algorithm employs complex-valued hybrid dealiasing via a new implicit packing technique, which packs real-valued data into complex-valued arrays and performs all unpacking and packing operations implicitly in the transformed domain. Multidimensional versions of both algorithms are obtained by recursive decomposition into lower-dimensional convolutions.
  Based on these algorithms, efficient routines to compute convolutions of equal-length input arrays are implemented in the open-source FFTW++ library. These routines outperform the standard method of explicit zero padding in one, two, and three dimensions. In many cases, implicit packing without hybrid dealiasing is nearly as fast as the general hybrid dealiasing algorithm, particularly when multithreading.

Abstract: Structure-preserving discontinuous Galerkin (DG) methods typically improve the robustness of high order simulations of real fluids. In addition to conservation, key structures include the preservation of pressure equilibrium and satisfaction of at least one entropy inequality. In this work, we investigate conservative discretizations using exactly pressure equilibrium conserving (EPEC) and approximately pressure equilibrium conserving (APEC) flux differencing DG formulations, as well as entropy stable formulations through the use of minimally dissipative corrections for non-ideal equations of state (EOS).
  We introduce an analysis of EPEC schemes and a new procedure for designing such fluxes based on a generalization of Tadmor's shuffle condition. We also analyze APEC DG schemes and show that the incorporation of dissipative interface penalization terms does not significantly increase pressure equilibrium errors, especially at higher orders of approximation. Finally, we observe that when combined with APEC flux differencing formulations, entropy correction improves robustness for under-resolved solutions and long-time simulations.

### [18] [Comment on arXiv:2106.08363v3 [math.NA], E. Abreu, A. Espirito Santo, W. Lambert, and J. Perez, Convergence of a Lagrangian--Eulerian scheme by a weak asymptotic analysis for one-dimensional hyperbolic problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14520)

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文是一篇评论性论文，指出E. Abreu等人2023年发表在Numer. Methods PDE上的文章中所宣称的"no-flow curves"（无流曲线）并非其创新，而是Douglas, Pereira和Yeh（DPY）2000年提出的Eulerian-Lagrangian局部守恒方法的时空积分曲线。作者逐方程证明了二者的同一性，并指出后续多篇论文（2025、2026年）存在术语和归属引用不当的问题，但未质疑后续的离散数值贡献本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该评论旨在纠正科学文献中的归属问题：Abreu等人2023年的论文将DPY（2000）早已引入的"no-flow curves"（由dsigma/dt = H(u)/u定义）作为新概念提出，其摘要声称该对象由作者引入，而其正文又称DPY 2000年论文首次引入时空局部守恒。这种自相矛盾的归属和随后的引用缺失需要被澄清和记录。

Method: 作者采用逐方程比较的方法（"equation by equation"），展示Abreu等人的no-flow曲线与DPY方法的Eulerian-Lagrangian时空积分曲线在数学上完全等价。具体论证包括：(1) no-flow区域即DPY方法中积分曲线所围成的管状区域；(2) no-flow曲线的标量前向追踪形式出现在Mancuso、Pereira和de Souza 2007年的论文中；(3) 指出关键数学事实：H'(u)与H(u)/u仅对线性通量恒等，只有后者才能使横向质量通量消失；(4) 通过全文语料库（full-text corpus）追踪术语演变和归属变化。

Result: 作者证明了Abreu等人的"no-flow curves"与DPY方法中的时空积分曲线在数学上是同一对象。发现2025和2026年发表的论文使用相同构造时引用了较晚的文献而未引用DPY；另一篇2026年的论文给出混合归属——称no-flow曲线是DPY积分曲线的"扩展"，但其自身的零通量定义和比值ODE表明二者恒等。作者明确表示不挑战后续的离散数值贡献（模型、格式和分析的扩展），仅针对连续曲线的归属问题。

Conclusion: 作者的核心结论是：问题不在于移动控制体积本身（这是通用的做法），而在于用哪条曲线来移动它。no-flow曲线是DPY方法的既有概念，Abreu等人的论文当归属性存在不当。该评论通过逐方程比对的严谨方式厘清了这一科学发现的优先权归属，同时承认后续论文在数值离散层面的原创贡献不受影响。

Abstract: This Comment concerns arXiv:2106.08363v3 [math.NA] by E. Abreu, A. Espirito Santo, W. Lambert and J. Perez, published in Numer. Methods Partial Differential Equations 39 (2023) 2400-2443. That article builds its scheme on space-time control volumes whose lateral boundaries, called "no-flow curves", solve dsigma/dt = H(u)/u and are presented as new. We show, equation by equation, that this object is the space-time integral curve of the locally conservative Eulerian-Lagrangian method of Douglas, Pereira and Yeh [Comput. Geosci. 4 (2000) 1-40], that the no-flow region is the tube those curves bound, and that its scalar forward-tracked form appears in Mancuso, Pereira and de Souza [TEMA 8 (2007) 269-276, 277-286]. At issue is not moving a control volume, which is generic, but which curve moves it: H'(u) and H(u)/u coincide identically only for a linear flux, and only the latter makes lateral mass flux vanish. The commented article itself calls the 2000 paper the first to introduce space-time local conservation, with an integral tube bounded by integral curves, while its abstract calls the same object introduced by the authors. A full-text corpus documents the changing terminology and attribution. Papers published in 2025 and 2026 use the same construction while citing later work but not DPY. Another 2026 paper gives mixed attribution: it calls the no-flow curve an "extension" of the DPY integral curve, although its own zero-flux definition and ratio ODE show identity, and it states that diffusion and dispersion do not modify the defining vector field. It extends the model, scheme and analysis, not the continuous curve. The later discrete contributions are not challenged.

</details>

### [19] [Fixed angle inverse scattering with non-constant velocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13670)

TL;DR: 本文研究变声速波动方程的反演问题，证明通过测量有限个平面波及其补解（complementary solutions）的边界数据，可以唯一恢复具有时不变系数的二阶双曲型算子最高阶项的未知系数。该结果改进并推广了先前将黎曼度量生成的波算子与欧氏度量生成的波算子进行比较的刚性结果〔10,11〕。关键创新是引入补解概念，并通过传输方程和Carleman估计从边界数据提取内部未知系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的刚性结果（文献[10,11]）只能将黎曼度量生成的波算子与欧氏度量生成的波算子进行比较，限制较大。本文希望将其推广到任意两个二阶双曲型算子之间的比较，在更一般的情形下（含变声速）实现系数的唯一恢复，同时只需对其中一个算子施加伪凸性、无焦散以及张成等几何条件。

Method: (1) 引入补解（complementary solution）概念：即对由入射平面波生成的广义平面波解，构造一个在跨界面时光滑延拓的解。(2) 利用补解在界面处满足的传输方程，将内部未知系数与边界数据联系起来。(3) 通过对波算子建立一系列Carleman估计，从边界数据逐步提取传输方程中的未知系数，从而反演内部系数。

Result: 证明了在有限个平面波及其补解的边界数据测量下，可以唯一恢复二阶双曲型算子最高阶项的未知系数（时不变）。特别地，若一个算子声速接近常数，另一个可为任意算子。该结果超越了先前仅能与欧氏度量比较的刚性结果。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入补解这一新概念并结合Carleman估计技术，建立了一种更通用的形式确定逆问题解法，允许在两个一般二阶双曲型算子之间进行比较性反演，显著扩展了逆边界值问题在该领域的适用范围。

Abstract: In this article, we study formally determined inverse problems for wave equations in the presence of a variable sound speed. We prove that by measuring the boundary data of finitely many plane waves and their complementary solutions, one can uniquely recover the unknown coefficients of the highest order terms of a second order hyperbolic operator with time independent coefficients. This improves earlier rigidity results in [10], [11] which compared the wave operator generated by a Riemannian metric with the wave operator generated by the Euclidean metric. We compare two general second order hyperbolic operators with time independent coefficients, with the same lower order terms. However, we require the geometry associated with one of the operators to satisfy a pseudoconvexity condition, a no-caustics condition, and a spanning condition. In particular one of the operators could be a wave operator with the sound speed close to a constant and the other operator could be arbitrary.
  To prove the results, we introduce the notion of a complementary solution for a generalized plane wave solution generated by an incoming plane wave. The complementary solution extends smoothly, across an interface, the generalized plane wave. The unknown coefficients appear in a transport equation at the interface. We show that the unknown coefficients in the interior can be extracted from this transport equation, from the boundary data, via a sequence of Carleman estimates for the wave operator.

</details>


### [20] [Regularized distance in space forms and its application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13704)
*Xiaodong Cao,Ling Xiao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为空间形式N^n(K)中的星形区域构造了正则化距离函数，并导出了其Hessian的显式公式（用边界主曲率表示）。作为应用，利用这些正则化距离函数构造障碍函数，证明了在环域上退化σ_k方程在适当边界条件下存在容许的C^{1,1}解。

Abstract: We consider the nonlinear Schrödinger equation in one dimension with nonlinearities of type $|u|^αu$ for any power $α>0$ and review two different methods for obtaining solutions, namely, local well-posedness, with initial data either in $L^2$ or $H^1$, or in the weighted subspace of $H^1$. One approach is based on the Strichartz estimates, and thus, $H^1$ well-posedness typically holds for nonlinearities with power $α\geq 1$. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the set of minimizers is orbitally stable under the Eulerian dynamics. The absence of spatial scaling and horizontal translation invariance necessitates novel strategies: on the strip, we refine a concentration-compactness argument to prove strict subadditivity; on weak finite volume domains, we bypass the need for subadditivity by exploiting the inherent decay rate $q$ of the domain to enforce compactness. This work synthesizes the approaches of \cite{abe2022stability}, \cite{Abe2025StabilityOL}, \cite{abe2025existence}, and \cite{Dong2026StabilityOV} into a comprehensive framework, significantly expanding the scope of provably stable vortex structures.

Motivation: 传统无限维逆问题分析假设连续数据，但实际应用只有有限个离散测量。近期确定性方法虽能保证从有限测量中稳定重建d维模型类中的未知量，但对严重不适定问题（如Calderón问题和逆散射），所需测量数可能随d指数增长。作者希望找到只需少量测量的方案，以解决这一实际瓶颈。

Method: 利用前向映射的解析几何性质，证明当无限数据问题在模型类上具有注入性时，通过随机采样标量测量，2d+1个测量足以确定未知量（几乎必然）。核心思想是将随机测量的唯一可辨识性与前向映射的解析结构联系起来，利用随机采样的概率性质绕过确定性构造在测量数量上的指数瓶颈。

Result: 证明了2d+1个随机标量测量即可几乎必然地唯一确定d维模型类中的未知量，前提是无限数据问题在该模型类上可注入。将该结果应用于Calderón问题（涵盖无限维和有限维边界采样）以及随机采样远场值下的逆介质散射问题。

Conclusion: 随机采样能够将严重不适定逆问题所需的测量数从指数级骤降至线性级（2d+1），但前提是仅要求精确的可辨识性而非稳定性。这为实际应用中降低测量成本提供了重要理论保证，同时也指出了随机采样在唯一性方面的优势与可能需要额外稳定机制之间的权衡。

Abstract: While infinite-dimensional inverse problems are traditionally analyzed assuming continuous data, practical applications rely on finitely many discrete measurements. Recent deterministic approaches establish that unknowns belonging to a $d$-dimensional model class can be stably recovered from finitely many measurements. However, for severely ill-posed problems, such as the Calderón problem and inverse scattering, the known constructions may require a number of measurements that is exponential in $d$. We show that random sampling reduces this count dramatically if one asks only for exact identifiability. By exploiting the analytic geometry of the forward maps, we prove that, whenever the infinite-data problem is injective on the model class, $2d+1$ random scalar measurements determine the unknown uniquely, almost surely. Applications are given to the Calderón problem, with both infinite- and finite-dimensional boundary sampling, and to inverse medium scattering from randomly sampled far-field values.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单调优化是涵盖单调函数的广义非凸问题，常用POA求解，但POA在描述可行集的顶点数量上可能随迭代呈指数增长，导致内存占用大且子程序开销高，扩展性差。

Method: 提出三项算法改进：(1)广义锚点选择，产生最优平衡单调性切割；(2)放宽的最优性条件，无需连续性假设即可保证有限终止；(3)矢量化变体，并发处理多个节点。此外开发了一种基于树的高效实现，加速POA核心子程序并以紧凑方式存储外近似。

Result: 数值实验表明改进方法较标准POA实现了数量级的速度提升，并在现有POA变体失败的算例上也能求解成功。

Abstract: Monotonic optimisation is a broad class of non-convex problems formulated in terms of monotone functions. Such problems are commonly solved via the polyblock outer-approximation algorithm (POA), a branch-and-bound method that iteratively refines a rectangular outer-approximation of the feasible set. POA scales poorly, however: the number of vertices needed to describe the approximation can grow exponentially, leading to large memory requirements and increasingly expensive subroutines. To address these limitations, we propose three algorithmic improvements: a generalised anchor selection that yields an optimal balanced monotonicity cut, a relaxed optimality condition that guarantees finite termination without continuity assumptions, and a vectorised variant that processes multiple nodes concurrently. We further develop an efficient tree-based implementation, which accelerates POA's core subroutines while storing the outer-approximation compactly. Numerical experiments show that these improvements yield order-of-magnitude speed-ups over standard POA and solve problems on which existing variants fail. Finally, we introduce polyblocks, an open-source Python package implementing the proposed algorithms alongside a framework for developing new POA variants, available at https://github.com/RashwanA/polyblocks.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 首先利用有限情景VaR-CVaR恒等式将原问题转化为凸组合集上的惩罚化DC规划；随后提出投影惯性Boosted Difference-of-Convex Functions Algorithm（iBDCA），该算法融合惯性外推加速、目标函数防护机制和增强线搜索策略，在非凸非光滑情况下保留了标准的投影DC框架；理论分析涵盖方法的良定义性、目标序列单调递减性、聚点的临界性以及局部R-线性收敛率。

Result: 理论结果证明iBDCA产生的目标序列单调递减且所有聚点均为惩罚化DC问题的临界点；在局部无并列条件下，整序列收敛到临界点并具有局部R-线性收敛速率。数值实验显示iBDCA在匹配的问题实例上优于DCA和标准BDCA，样本外回测相较于等权和买入持有基准展示了实现收益、风险、交易成本和经验VaR控制之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为带VaR约束的多期投资组合优化问题提供了一种理论完备且计算高效的求解框架，所提出的iBDCA算法在收敛保证上优于现有方法，并通过数值和回测实验验证了其在实际投资决策中的有效性。

Abstract: We study a multi-period portfolio optimization problem with finite-scenario Value-at-Risk (VaR) constraints, transaction costs, and diversification regularization. Using a finite-scenario VaR--CVaR identity, we derive a penalized difference-of-convex (DC) formulation over the underlying convex portfolio set. To solve the resulting nonsmooth and nonconvex problem, we propose a projected inertial Boosted Difference-of-Convex Functions Algorithm (iBDCA) that combines inertial extrapolation, an objective safeguard, and a boosted line search. We prove that the method is well defined, generates a monotonically decreasing objective sequence, and has only critical accumulation points for the penalized DC problem. Under a local no-ties condition at an accumulation point, we further establish whole sequence convergence with a local \(R\)-linear rate. Numerical experiments compare iBDCA with DCA and standard BDCA on matched problem instances. Out-of-sample backtests additionally include equal-weight and buy-and-hold benchmarks to illustrate the trade-offs among realized return, risk, transaction costs, and empirical VaR control.

</details>

Abstract: This work studies a class of zero-sum stochastic linear quadratic dynamic games (LQDGs) under partial and asymmetric information. Information asymmetry introduces fundamental challenges related to \textit{belief representation} and \textit{theory of mind}, where players must impute belief states and estimates of other players to inform their strategies. Existing work highlights the difficulty of applying dynamic programming-like decomposition approach to these problems. An alternative approach based on \textit{best response dynamics} is proposed, which provides insights into belief representation and theory of mind challenges. Explicit expressions for each player's best response within the class of pure linear dynamic output feedback control strategies are derived, where the internal state dimension of each control is an integer multiple of the system state dimension. As players iteratively update their best responses, they form increasingly higher-order belief states, leading to infinite-dimensional internal states. However, numerical results reveal that the game's value converges after only a few iterations, suggesting that higher-order belief states provide vanishing benefit. This work further conducts numerical experiments to analyze the impact of asymmetric beliefs, belief orders, relative controllability and observability, and direct feed-through on a linear quadratic pursuit-evasion game's value.

</details>

Result: 建立了统一的区域信息滤波与控制框架；推导了贝尔曼方程及平稳最优策略存在性条件；提出了同时保留连续和原子分量的类相关有限维近似方案；给出了近似滤波误差与值函数误差之间的抽象界；通过直方图近似的参考模型验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为阈值、量化、删失、事件触发和间歇观测等区域信息机制下的部分可观测随机控制提供了统一的数学基础，通过信息状态变换实现了问题向完全可观测马尔可夫决策过程的转化，并提供了实际可行的有限维近似途径。

Abstract: We develop a filtering and optimal-control framework for partially observable stochastic systems in which each observation identifies a class of a finite measurable partition of the hidden state space. This structure covers regional-information mechanisms associated with threshold, quantized, censored, event-triggered, and intermittent observations, and allows observable classes with atomic, continuous, or mixed components. The formulation is constructed first at the level of measures: for each observable class, we define a class-restricted unnormalized conditional measure, and the posterior distribution is obtained by normalizing with its predictive probability. Based on this recursion, we introduce an information state consisting of the observed class and the conditional measure supported on it, thereby transforming the original problem into a fully observable Markov decision process. We formulate the discounted-cost criterion, derive the Bellman equation, and establish conditions for the existence of stationary optimal policies. To address the infinite-dimensional nature of the information space, we propose class-dependent finite-dimensional approximations capable of preserving both continuous components and atomic masses. We also derive an abstract bound linking the error of the approximate filter to the error of the value function. A reference model illustrates the construction through histogram-based approximations

Abstract: We establish nondegeneracy and normality conditions for optimal control problems with a single nonregular mixed inequality constraint, where purely finitely additive set functions (charges) appear as multipliers. We first prove for a model problem nonregular at a single instant that degeneration is intrinsic and unavoidable: every admissible process satisfies the necessary conditions degenerately via a pure charge, and normalized multipliers of natural regular approximations admit no weak$^*$ limit in $L^\infty([0,1];\mathbb{R})^*$, yielding only pure charges under every generalized limit, in the sense of Banach limits. Motivated by this analysis, we propose four verifiable conditions that progressively guarantee nondegenerate multipliers, the vanishing of the pure charge, normality, and explicit bounds on charge mass. Furthermore, we show that at a nonregular terminal instant, a balance identity ties the charge mass to the cost multiplier, deciding between complete abnormality and normality. We provide examples confirming the realizability and discussing the limits of each condition.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将一阶优化建模为最小时间控制问题：迭代点视为状态，由历史梯度构成的更新视为控制，梯度范数不超过预设容差的点集构成目标集。最小查询次数构成一个值函数。在强凸二次情形下，通过离散Pontryagin最小值原理推导出共轭梯度迭代，值函数对应可控性指标；在非二次情形下，引入Hessian生成的可达张成子空间（替代可控性矩阵），将可行性转化为可达性问题。文中还给出一个可检验的条件，证明某些实例所需的步数可少于其在极小点处二次模型的可控性指标，且该差距可随维度无界增大。

Abstract: We formulate first-order optimization as a minimum-time control problem. The iterate is the state, the update, a combination of the gradients observed so far, is the control, and the points with gradient norm at most a prescribed tolerance form the target set. For a fixed objective and start, the minimum number of oracle queries needed to reach the target is a value function: it measures the complexity of the instance, not the worst case over a class. On a strongly convex quadratic, the conjugate gradient iterates emerge from the discrete Pontryagin conditions, and the value is a controllability index. Beyond the quadratic, a Hessian-generated reachable span replaces the controllability matrix, and feasibility becomes a reachability question: whether a critical point can be reached at all, and in how many steps, is read from the span. Curvature is a resource: a checkable condition certifies that an instance needs fewer steps than the controllability index of its quadratic model at the minimizer, and the gap can grow without bound with the dimension. The value is thus a benchmark for the intrinsic difficulty of each instance, against which any first-order method can be measured.

</details>

Abstract: We study clean energy transition planning for campus-scale integrated electricity-heat systems under both strategic level and operational level uncertainties. We formulate a multi-stage stochastic mixed-integer program that jointly optimizes investment and operational decisions for renewable generation, storage, and heat-transfer technologies whose costs and efficiencies evolve stochastically across stages. To account for short-term operational uncertainty, we further derive a robust reformulation based on box uncertainty sets for demand, renewable generation, and heat-transfer performance. To solve the resulting large-scale model, we develop a Benders decomposition algorithm with partial non-anticipativity relaxation. Investment variables and their non-anticipativity constraints are retained in the master problem while operational variables are assigned to scenario-wise subproblems where their non-anticipativity constraints are relaxed. Non-anticipativity of operational variables is restored only at termination through a smaller linear program. We prove that this correction step can only increase the objective function value by a finite bound. We improve the computational performance of the algorithm developed with valid inequalities and a two-phase cut-addition strategy. Using the proposed approach, we solve the Middle East Technical University campus case study at high temporal resolution within a reasonable computational budget, which is not otherwise possible with the extensive form or the classical Benders decomposition. We evaluate the resulting investment plans through rolling-horizon Monte Carlo simulations with an out-of-sample analysis and demonstrate that the added robustness can significantly improve the operational reliability of the transition plans with a moderate increase in total cost.

Abstract: Modern online retailers leverage networks of distributed warehouses to rapidly fulfill customer orders. We study a problem of jointly deciding (i) how to allocate inventories across warehouses in the network subject to warehouse capacity and product supply constraints, (ii) how to dynamically select personalized product assortments based on customer preferences and location, as well as real-time stock levels, and (iii) which warehouse to use to fulfill the product chosen by the customer to maximize expected profit. In our model, the firm chooses an inventory placement across warehouses at the start of the selling horizon. Customers of different types then arrive at discrete time periods over a finite time horizon according to a known distribution. When offered an assortment, each customer type chooses at most one product according to a discrete choice model. The firm then chooses a feasible warehouse from which to fulfill the chosen product, depleting its stock and earning a profit that depends on the product, customer type, and warehouse choice. We develop approximation algorithms and policies with provable theoretical guarantees and strong empirical performance. Under general probabilistic choice models, we establish asymptotically optimal approximation algorithms as warehouse capacities and product supplies scale. Under the multinomial logit (MNL) choice model, we obtain constant-factor approximation algorithms, with approximation factors ranging from 0.080 in the most general setting to 0.199 under stationarity and unlimited product supplies, which means that only warehouse capacity constraints are present. To the best of our knowledge, these results provide the first provably efficient algorithms for the joint inventory placement, assortment personalization, and order fulfillment problem.

Abstract: We consider a processing network model with $m$ job classes or buffers, exogenous input flows into some classes, $n$ processing activities, and $p$ servers. Each activity is either a specified server processing jobs of a specified class, or a fictional input server delivering jobs of a specified class; jobs change class in Markovian fashion after completing service. A standard multiclass queueing network, with its one-to-one correspondence between job classes and activities, is a special case, but our general model allows two or more ways to process a given class, and some or all input flows may be turned away at the system manager's discretion.
  Costs are linear: a holding cost per time unit for each class $i$ job in the system, and a rejection penalty for each class $i$ arrival denied access $(i=1,\ldots,m)$. The system manager makes input control, job routing, and order-of-service decisions to minimize expected discounted costs over an infinite horizon.
  We formulate an approximating Brownian control problem (BCP) whose state space is the $m$-dimensional nonnegative orthant; control is a drift vector chosen from a bounded polyhedral set, based on dynamic state observations. Using recently developed computational methods, the BCP can be solved numerically in dimensions up to at least $m=50$, and we explain how the numerical solution is translated into an implementable control policy for the queueing system of original interest.
  Previous work on heavy traffic diffusion approximations suggests that this policy is nearly optimal in the heavy traffic parameter regime, and numerical examples support that conjecture. We also discuss its advantage over an alternative approach, featured in our previous work, where the BCP is replaced by a lower-dimensional "equivalent workload formulation" that is computationally efficient but difficult to interpret in the network of original interest.

Abstract: Many control problems are classically formulated using integral costs that capture the cumulative performance of a system. Peak or worst-case behavior is captured via supremum or $L^\infty$-costs in another variety of control problems. When both cumulative and peak performance are important, it is natural to consider objective functions that combine the two costs. Although each criterion is well studied, their combination has not been explored extensively and we precisely work on such control problems.
  Towards establishing the existence, we first consider the relaxed framework, where control is considered using probability distributions. Using the well-known compactness and convexity properties of such control spaces, we establish the existence of an optimal relaxed control---we eventually establish the existence of an $ε$-optimal pure (or classical) control, for every $ε> 0.$
  Despite these existence results, computing optimal policies remains challenging due to the non-smoothness introduced by the supremum term, and it is not clear whether the dynamic programming principle holds for our combined problem. To address this, we introduce a family of smooth approximations that yield standard control problems with well-defined optimal (pure) solutions. Using Maximum Theorem, we establish that the solutions of the smooth problems among pure controls form $ε$-optimal for the original problem, with $ε$ tending to zero as the smoothing parameter converges to zero.
  Finally using the methods proposed in this paper, we study a queueing problem to illustrate (among others) that the required trade-off between peak congestion levels and cumulative performance can be achieved.

</details>

Abstract: We study optimal control problems for incompressible fluids in two-dimensional domains with a cold boundary and internal heat sources and sinks. Given a kinetic energy budget, measured by the square of a nondimensional Péclet number $\mathrm{Pe}$, the goal is to maximize a cooling efficiency inversely proportional to the mean square gradient of the fluid's temperature. Using Lagrange duality, we formulate a well-posed dual problem whose solution yields an upper bound on the maximum cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. We then numerically approximate the dual problem using a convergent hierarchy of semidefinite programs obtained via discretization. We illustrate this computational approach on optimal cooling problems in a square and in an annulus, explaining also how problem symmetries can be exploited to reduce computational complexity. Finally, we construct admissible points for the dual problem to obtain new analytical upper bounds on the optimal cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. Specifically, we prove that $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe}) \lesssim \mathrm{Pe}^{2}$ for arbitrary domains and heat distributions, and that $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})\lesssim \mathrm{Pe}^{2}/ \ln^2\mathrm{Pe}$ for cooling flows in disks and annuli with heat source/sink distributions with a positive azimuthal average. These results generalize and improve known efficiency bounds for energy-constrained cooling flows in a disk.

Abstract: Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization (DRO) is a versatile and widely adopted framework for decision-making under uncertainty, yet its standard deterministic reformulations generally contain non-convex inner subproblems that are challenging to solve. We derive a priori suboptimality bounds in stylized settings and, for the general case, a posteriori bounds obtained by applying a similar heuristic to a dual formulation. Numerical experiments on a multi-item newsvendor and an appointment scheduling problem show that the shrinkage path heuristic attains 85-110% (resp. 45-70%) of the out-of-sample performance improvements of Wasserstein DRO over SAA, at a fraction of the computational cost.

Motivation: ADMM是近二十年备受关注的里程碑式算法。虽然两块ADMM具有成熟的理论收敛保证，但直接扩展到三块可能不收敛（已有反例）。然而，第三块约束为恒等映射这一特殊子类的情况一直是悬而未决的问题——现有文献既无收敛性证明也无反例。填补这一理论空白是本文的核心动机。

Method: 作者利用Codex配合GPT-5.6 Sol构建了一个显式有理反例候选，并通过分段仿射约化路径进行验证，精确检查确认该实例产生周期为66的有界非收敛轨道。在此基础上，研究了乘子松弛对收敛恢复的影响：在固定实例层面，特定的小对偶步长可以恢复收敛；但不存在对所有实例都有效的正相对步长。此外，使用Kimi Code与Kimi K3模型在不提供Codex候选或项目引导的情况下，沿另一路径得到周期为23的精确局部吸引证书，可转化为等价的全恒等映射实例。

Result: 回答了未解问题：直接三块ADMM即使在前两块为强凸二次函数且第三块为恒等映射时仍可能不收敛（周期66反例）。乘子松弛可以在逐实例层面以小对偶步长恢复收敛，但不存在全类统一的收敛恢复步长。不同AI研究工具配置会塑造所探索的数学对象和所追求的证明证书类型。

Conclusion: 本文给出否定答案，证明了三块ADMM在第三块恒等且前两块强凸二次这一受限子类下仍可能发散（不收敛）。该发现填补了该子类长期悬而未决的理论空白，同时揭示了乘子松弛恢复收敛的局限性与AI辅助数学研究工具的路径依赖性。

Abstract: The alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), as a landmark algorithm, has attracted tremendous research attention and extensive practical applications over the past two decades. It is well known that, although the two-block ADMM enjoys well-established theoretical convergence guarantees, its direct extension to the three-block case may fail to converge, as demonstrated by existing counterexamples [5]. However, to the best of our knowledge, the case in which the third constraint block is the identity remains unresolved: the existing literature gives neither a general convergence proof nor a counterexample for this subclass. In this paper, we give a negative answer: direct three-block ADMM may fail even when the first two blocks are strongly convex quadratics. Using Codex with GPT-5.6 Sol, we construct an explicit rational counterexample candidate and verify it along a piecewise-affine reduction path; exact checks show that direct three-block ADMM on this instance produces a bounded nonconvergent orbit of period 66. Within the same Codex workflow, we further guide a study of multiplier relaxation and clarify when convergence can be restored at the fixed-instance and class levels: a problem-dependent small dual step can restore convergence, whereas no positive relative step works uniformly over the whole class. Furthermore, we also test the recent Kimi Code with Kimi K3 model without the Codex candidate or project-specific route guidance; along a different path it produces an exact locally attracting period-23 certificate, convertible to an equivalent all-identity instance. The comparison suggests that different research-harness configurations can shape the mathematical objects explored and the certificates pursued.

</details>


### [48] [Residual-Controlled Douglas--Rachford Splitting for Differentiable Solver Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14470)
*Kang Liu,Jianchen Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出RCDRS（Residual-Controlled Douglas–Rachford Splitting），一种用于锥线性规划的可微分求解器层。它将展开式求解器视为反馈控制的动态系统，通过因果控制器自适应调整松弛和目标驱动参数，同时保持Douglas–Rachford分裂的投影分裂结构。理论上证明了每个固定可行块仍是平均松弛DRS算子并具有有限步不动点残差界；实验表明其在混合锥基准和工程应用中改善了求解质量、可行性及下游决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可微分求解器层将约束优化嵌入端到端学习系统中，但固定深度的展开方法必须在求解质量、可行性和计算预算之间进行权衡，缺乏自适应机制来动态调整求解过程中的参数。

Method: 将展开式求解器建模为反馈控制的动态系统，采用因果控制器自适应调节松弛参数和目标驱动参数，保留Douglas–Rachford分裂的投影分裂结构；理论上分析固定可行块为平均松弛DRS算子并给出有限步不动点残差界，还将安全时变滚动视为极限平均算子上的可和扰动，并从最终分裂状态恢复终端原始-对偶诊断量。

Result: 在混合锥基准和工程应用上的实验表明，RCDRS相比基线方法在求解质量、可行性和下游决策性能方面均有所提升。

Conclusion: RCDRS通过反馈控制思想为可微分锥规划求解器层提供了自适应参数调节机制，在理论上保证了算子性质和收敛残差界，并在实践中验证了其在求解质量和下游任务上的优势。

Abstract: Differentiable solver layers embed constrained optimization into end-to-end learning systems, but fixed-depth unrolling must trade off solution quality, feasibility, and computational budget. We propose Residual-Controlled Douglas--Rachford Splitting (RCDRS), a differentiable solver layer for conic linear programs. RCDRS treats an unrolled solver as a feedback-controlled dynamical system, where a causal controller adapts the relaxation and objective-drive parameters while preserving the projection-splitting structure of Douglas--Rachford splitting. Theoretically, we show that each fixed admissible block remains an averaged relaxed DRS operator and admits finite-step fixed-point residual bounds. We further analyze safeguarded time-varying rollouts as summable perturbations of a limiting averaged operator, and recover terminal primal-dual diagnostics from the final splitting state. Experiments on mixed-cone benchmarks and engineering applications show that RCDRS improves solution quality, feasibility and downstream decision performance. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RC-DRS-180C/.

</details>


### [49] [On quantitative sufficient second-order optimality conditions for elliptic optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14525)
*Francisco Fuica,Nicolai Jork*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对无Tikhonov正则化的分布式最优控制问题，提出了一种定量最优性条件，该条件确保关于线性化状态L²范数的强制性，并进而证明二阶条件关于状态和跟踪数据扰动的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无Tikhonov正则化的最优控制问题中，二阶变分是关于线性化状态的二次型，其曲率系数函数可能消失或改变符号，这使得建立强制性条件变得困难，进而影响二阶最优性条件的稳定性分析。

Method: 针对受半线性椭圆方程约束、带箱约束的分布式最优控制问题，提出一个定量条件，该条件蕴含关于线性化状态L²范数的强制性（coercivity）。

Result: 所提出的定量条件保证了二次型的强制性，并由此证明了二阶最优性条件对状态和跟踪数据扰动的稳定性。

Conclusion: 即使在缺少Tikhonov正则化导致曲率系数可能消失或变号的情况下，仍可通过定量条件确保强制性，从而为二阶条件的稳健性提供理论保证。

Abstract: In this paper, a quantitative condition for optimality for distributed optimal control problems with box-constraints that are subject to a semilinear elliptic equation is considered. An important property of the investigated optimal control problems is the absence of a Tikhonov regularization. It is well known that at a given control, the second variation is a quadratic form in the linearized state, and its curvature coefficient function may vanish or change sign. We present a quantitative condition that implies coercivity with respect to the $L^2$-norm of the linearized-states. As a consequence, the stability of the second-order condition under perturbations of the states and the tracking data is shown.

</details>

### [50] [L-FNO: Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator for Stochastic Event Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13562)
*Songhee Kang,Jihoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出洛伦兹傅里叶神经算子（L-FNO），一种结合FNO风格协变量路径、洛伦兹谱核的历史依赖激发机制和基于似然的训练目标的随机神经算子，用于建模稀有、突发且自激发的随机事件动态。在八个合成点过程基准和三个真实数据集上，L-FNO在事件似然、校准诊断和稀有事件检测上均优于回归式和基于似然的神经算子基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代运营系统即使在常规条件下也面临不确定性，其中稀有、突发且自激发的事件既受外生协变量影响，也受内生事件动态驱动。标准神经算子通常被训练为回归式的函数到函数映射模型，而非条件强度估计器，这限制了它们在稀疏事件场景中的适用性。因此需要一种能够预测随机事件动态的神经算子模型。

Method: L-FNO包含三个核心组件：1）FNO风格的协变量路径，用于处理外生协变量；2）洛伦兹谱核，用于建模历史依赖的激发机制（即内生事件动态）；3）基于似然的训练目标，将模型训练为条件强度估计器而非回归模型。该方法将神经算子从确定性函数映射扩展为随机事件过程的生成模型。

Result: 在八个合成点过程基准和三个真实数据集（涵盖疾病暴发预测和半导体故障/缺陷检测）上，L-FNO在事件似然、校准诊断和稀有事件检测方面均优于回归式和基于似然的神经算子基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结构化谱记忆（structured spectral memory）和基于似然的学习为神经算子建模随机事件动态提供了有效的归纳偏置，验证了将神经算子扩展到条件强度估计框架的可行性和优势。

Abstract: Modern operational systems face uncertainty even in routine conditions, where rare, bursty, and self-exciting events emerge from both exogenous covariates and endogenous event dynamics. Standard neural operators are typically trained as regression-style function-to-function models rather than conditional-intensity estimators, limiting their suitability for sparse event regimes. We introduce the Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator (L-FNO), a stochastic neural operator that combines an FNO-style covariate path, Lorentzian spectral kernels for history-dependent excitation, and a likelihood-based training objective. We evaluate L-FNO on eight synthetic point-process benchmarks and three real-world datasets covering disease outbreak prediction and semiconductor fault or defect detection. L-FNO improves event likelihood, calibration diagnostics, and rare-event detection over regression- and likelihood-based neural operator baselines. These results show that structured spectral memory and likelihood-based learning provide effective inductive biases for neural operator models of stochastic event dynamics.

</details>


### [51] [Don't Claim Benchmark-Oriented Optimization Improves General Coding Capability -- Diverse Evaluation Is Required](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13566)
*Egor Shibaev,Vera Kudrevskaia,Timur Galimzyanov,Mikhail Evtikhiev,Ana Terna,Rastislav Rabatin,Timur Kudashev,Timofey Bryksin,Arina Puchkova,Patrik Bartak,Egor Bogomolov,Sergey Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前仅用少量编码基准（如SWE-bench和LiveCodeBench）评估大模型编码能力存在"意义鸿沟"，即基准分数与实际通用编码能力间的差距。作者通过自建的Django案例基准套件验证发现：基于SWE-bench轨迹的后训练模型在跨任务上几乎无迁移，基准排名泛化失败。因此主张采用差异化评估策略，并呼吁建立能力分类体系和持续维护基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有论文、模型卡片和博客普遍用少量编码基准分数当作广泛编码能力的证据，导致基准优化产生任务特定表现，测量分数与通用编码能力声明之间存在意义差距，且缺乏系统性验证。

Method: 作者构建了一个基于Django框架的案例研究基准套件，评估在SWE-bench轨迹上后训练的基础模型和检查点，测试其跨任务迁移能力，并对比SWE-bench、LiveCodeBench及自建Django任务上的表现，同时对单个Django模态进行微调实验验证迁移效果。

Result: 发现基准排名经常无法泛化：后训练检查点几乎没有跨任务迁移；SWE-bench优化在自建任务和LiveCodeBench上仅产生有限或没有收益；对单个Django模态的微调同样无法迁移到其他任务。

Conclusion: 少量基准不足以在基准优化压力下评估多样化模型。建议采用差异化评估策略：前沿模型用整体评估、研究用多任务套件、窄任务应用用人在回路研究。同时呼吁构建能力分类体系、持续维护基准而非一次性发布，否则工程师和研究人员将基于不足证据做决策。

Abstract: Post-training papers, model cards, and blog posts often treat scores on a small set of coding benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench and LiveCodeBench) as evidence of broad coding capability, both for research artifacts and user-facing systems. We argue that optimization for these benchmarks leads to measuring task-specific performance, creating a meaning gap between measured scores and claims of general coding ability. We examine this gap with a Django-based case study benchmark suite we create.
  Evaluating foundation models and checkpoints post-trained on SWE-bench trajectories, we find that benchmark rankings frequently fail to generalize. Post-trained checkpoints show little cross-task transfer, and SWE-bench optimization yields limited or no gains on our tasks or on LiveCodeBench. Similarly, fine-tuning on individual Django modalities fails to transfer.
  We conclude that a small number of benchmarks is insufficient for evaluating diverse models under benchmark optimization pressure. We encourage the community to use differentiated evaluation - holistic assessment for frontier models, multi-task suites for research, and human-in-the-loop studies for narrow task applications. Finally, we argue for creating a capability taxonomy and sustained benchmark maintenance, rather than one-off benchmark releases. Without reliable evaluation standards, engineers and researchers using LLMs and agents have to rely on insufficient evidence to make research, development, and deployment decisions.

</details>


### [52] [Robust XGBoosting for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13590)
*Iris Aragón Mladosich,Christophe Croux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了XGBoost的稳健性问题，发现其性能会受垂直异常值和杠杆点的影响。为此，作者探索了基于M、S和τ估计量的替代损失函数，并提出了一种名为MM-XGBoost的两步方法，在稳健性和预测精度之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XGBoost虽然是一种非常流行且强大的预测方法，但其标准损失函数（二次损失）和Huber损失在存在垂直异常值和杠杆点时表现不佳。现有研究缺乏对XGBoost在数据污染下稳健性的系统分析，因此需要探索更稳健的损失函数。

Method: 作者在XGBoost框架中引入基于稳健回归中M估计量、S估计量和τ估计量的替代损失函数，并提出一个两步程序MM-XGBoost，通过组合这些稳健估计量来兼顾稳健性和预测精度。

Result: 实验结果表明，MM-XGBoost方法在稳健性与预测准确性之间提供了最佳的权衡，优于标准的二次损失和Huber损失以及其他替代损失函数。

Conclusion: MM-XGBoost作为一种两阶段稳健增强方法，能够有效抵御垂直异常值和杠杆点对XGBoost性能的影响，为鲁棒梯度提升提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: XGBoost is a very popular and powerful method for prediction. It iteratively fits simple decision trees to the residuals of the previous step. An efficient and scalable implementation is available. The standard loss function for XGBoost is the quadratic loss, but a Huber loss can also be used. In this paper, we study the robustness of XGBoost and show that its performance can be affected by vertical outliers and leverage points. To address this, we explore alternative loss functions, based on M-, S-, and τ -estimators from robust regression. Our results indicate that a two-step procedure, referred to as MM-XGBoost, provides the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [53] [Training-Free Knowledge Transfer Across Model Scales through Activation-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13596)
*Jiahe Fan,Si Chen,Yinghao Hou,Aiyuan Zhang,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出激活-剪枝-融合（APM）框架，实现在架构不匹配的跨尺度场景下将大模型（供体）的能力迁移到小模型（受体）中。APM通过激活图引导裁剪供体至受体架构，并用极小的插值系数注入受体模型，无需神经元级语义对齐，在16个基准上将3B受体的平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构模型融合旨在结合任务、初始化、架构或规模不同的模型，但现有研究鲜少探索跨尺度（cross-scale）这一场景，即在供体与受体存在显著架构不一致时，如何将强供体能力迁移至弱小受体。本文试图回答：在无需显式神经元级语义对齐的前提下，能否实现有用的能力迁移。

Method: APM方法包含三个核心步骤：（1）在供体上构建任务条件化的激活图（activation maps）；（2）基于激活图选择显著的层、隐藏维度、注意力头和MLP神经元，将大供体裁剪为与受体匹配的小架构；（3）以微插值系数（micro interpolation coefficient）将裁剪后的供体切片注入原始受体。该方法将供体视为浓缩功能组件的来源，而非要求精确的结构移植。

Result: 在覆盖推理、数学、代码生成、指令跟随和分类的16个基准上，APM将原始3B受体的平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%。具体地，RTE准确率从64.3%提升至82.3%，QNLI从52.3%提升至65.7%，BoolQ从70.8%提升至79.2%。进一步分析注入比率和序列多阶段融合表明，激活引导提取可提高供体切片质量，同时保持小比率融合机制。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当供体贡献足够浓缩且经过精心选择时，跨尺度异构融合即使没有显式语义对齐也能成功。该工作为异构模型融合提供了新的视角，即从精确结构移植转向功能组件的集中提取与融合。

Abstract: Heterogeneous model fusion seeks to combine models that differ in tasks, initializations, architectures, or scales. We study an underexplored cross-scale setting: improving a small recipient language model with a stronger donor despite substantial architectural mismatch. We ask whether useful capabilities can be transferred without explicit neuron-wise semantic alignment. Building on the observation that truncating a large model to a smaller architecture and injecting it with a tiny mixing weight can already improve the recipient, we propose Activation-Prune-Merge (APM), an activation-guided framework for cross-scale fusion. APM constructs task-conditioned activation maps on the donor, selects salient layers, hidden dimensions, attention heads, and MLP neurons to prune it to the recipient architecture, and injects the resulting donor slice into the original recipient using a micro interpolation coefficient. This formulation treats the donor as a source of concentrated functional components rather than requiring precise structural transplantation. Across 16 benchmarks spanning reasoning, mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and classification, APM improves the overall average accuracy from 55.5% to 60.6% over the original 3B recipient. RTE accuracy increases from 64.3% to 82.3%, QNLI from 52.3% to 65.7%, and BoolQ from 70.8% to 79.2%. Analyses of injection ratios and sequential multi-stage fusion further suggest that activation-guided extraction improves the quality of the transferable donor slice while preserving the small-ratio fusion regime. These results provide evidence that cross-scale heterogeneous fusion can succeed without explicit semantic alignment when the donor contribution is sufficiently concentrated and carefully selected.

</details>


### [54] [Hard Cases, Bad Labels: Testing Error Exposure and Error Location in Uncertainty Sampling Under Bounded Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13601)
*John Myron Uy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模配对种子实验（100个种子、9种噪声率、多个标注预算），系统测试了基于边距的不确定性采样在含噪标签下是否优于随机采样。结果发现：在干净标签下，不确定性采样在所有三个数据集上均提升归一化平衡准确率曲线下面积1.09至1.77个百分点；但难度依赖型噪声是否破坏这一优势取决于数据集（在乳腺癌数据上显著削弱，在另外两个数据集上无影响）；噪声位置的结构化效应未发现普遍性的额外惩罚。结论是：不确定性采样的鲁棒性高度依赖数据集、预算、噪声结构和评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性采样（Active Learning的经典方法）虽然能挑选信息量大的样本，但这些最不确定的样本可能恰好是最难正确标注的样本，导致标签错误率更高。此前缺乏系统性的因果分解实验来区分两种失效机制：一是获取了更多坏标签，二是错误集中出现在困难区域造成的额外危害。本研究旨在通过精心的实验设计（曝光匹配的RCN对照、难度依赖噪声、清洁标签延伸）来分离这两种解释，并评估不确定性采样在真实噪声条件下的实际价值。

Method: 使用三个公开二分类表格数据集，比较边距不确定性采样与随机采样。实验设计了三种标签条件：干净标签、随机分类噪声（RCN）、有界难度依赖噪声。采用100个配对种子、9种期望噪声率（0到0.30）、标注预算20至120（清洁标签延伸至400）。使用正则化逻辑回归，每个预算通过交叉验证重新选择正则化参数。关键方法创新包括：曝光匹配RCN对照（确保平均最终获取的噪声率一致性）以及多指标评估（归一化平衡准确率AUC、平均精度、固定假阳性率下的真阳性率）。

Result: 在干净标签下，不确定性采样在所有数据集上改善平衡准确率AUC达1.09至1.77个百分点。在乳腺癌数据集中，难度依赖噪声在8个测试噪声率中的6个上比RCN更严重地削弱了这种优势；但在其他两个数据集上没有任何噪声率下出现该效应。曝光匹配分析未发现结构化错误位置带来普遍性额外惩罚的一致证据。在干净的MAGIC数据上，虽然不确定采样提升了平衡准确率，但降低了平均精度和在固定假阳性率下的真阳性率，表明存在不同评估指标间的权衡。

Conclusion: 不确定性采样确实是标签高效的（在干净和多数噪声条件下提升学习效率），但其表现出的鲁棒性并非普遍成立，而是受数据集特性、标注预算、噪声结构以及评估指标的选择共同影响。论文挑战了单纯关注"是否获取了更多坏标签"的单一因果归因，主张应同时考虑噪声位置的结构化效应，且这种效应不具备跨数据集的普适性。

Abstract: Active learning can reduce labeling cost by selecting informative examples, but the most uncertain examples may also be the hardest to label correctly. This study tests whether uncertainty sampling fails because it acquires more corrupted labels or because errors concentrated in difficult regions are especially harmful. Margin-based uncertainty sampling is compared with random sampling under clean labels, random classification noise (RCN), and bounded difficulty-dependent noise on three public binary tabular datasets. The design uses 100 paired seeds, nine expected noise rates from 0 to 0.30, annotation budgets from 20 to 120, and logistic regression with regularization re-selected by cross-validation at every budget. An exposure-matched RCN control aligns mean final acquired corruption, while a clean-label extension reaches budget 400. Under clean labels, uncertainty sampling improved normalized balanced-accuracy area under the learning curve by 1.09 to 1.77 percentage points on all datasets. Difficulty-dependent noise reduced this advantage more than RCN at six of eight rates on Breast Cancer Wisconsin, but at no tested rate on Banknote Authentication or MAGIC Gamma Telescope. Exposure-matched analyses found no corrected evidence for a universal additional penalty from structured error location. On clean MAGIC data, uncertainty sampling improved balanced accuracy while reducing average precision and true-positive rate at fixed false-positive rates. Thus, uncertainty sampling was label-efficient, but its apparent robustness depended on dataset, budget, noise structure, and evaluation metric.

</details>


### [55] [Robust Dual-Model Collaborative Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13628)
*A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于核风险敏感均值p次幂准则的随机向量功能链接(RVFL)模型KRPRVFL，通过将标准最小二乘目标替换为KRP损失，并引入协作学习机制与核诱导特征映射，有效解决传统RVFL对噪声标签、异常值和数据不平衡敏感的问题，在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上展现出优于基线模型的准确率、鲁棒性和统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RVFL网络虽然具有轻量、快速、泛化能力强等优点，但其对噪声标签、异常值和数据不平衡极其敏感，严重制约了其在真实世界应用中的性能表现。现有方法缺乏对这些挑战的系统性解决方案。

Method: 1) 将标准最小二乘目标替换为基于核风险敏感均值p次幂(KRP)准则的损失函数，自适应降低受污染或不可靠样本在训练中的影响；2) 引入协作学习机制，实现模型组件间的自适应交互，增强复杂噪声环境下的鲁棒性；3) 利用核诱导特征映射捕捉非线性关系，无需显式隐层选择，保持效率与可扩展性。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的大量实验表明，KRPRVFL在准确率、鲁棒性和统计显著性方面一致性地优于基线模型，验证了其作为快速、可扩展且可靠分类解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: KRPRVFL成功将RVFL的计算效率与KRP准则的鲁棒性相结合，为对抗噪声标签、异常值和数据不平衡等挑战提供了一种高效、稳健且可扩展的分类框架，是实际应用中有竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: Random vector functional link (RVFL) networks are lightweight and fast neural models that offer efficient training and strong generalization through randomized hidden-layer weights and direct input-output connections. However, conventional RVFL models are sensitive to noisy labels, outliers, and imbalanced data, which limits their performance in real-world applications. To address these challenges, we propose the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power based RVFL (KRPRVFL) model, which integrates the computational efficiency of RVFL with the robustness of the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power (KRP) criterion. By replacing the standard least-squares objective with a KRP-based loss, KRPRVFL adaptively reduces the influence of corrupted or unreliable samples during training, resulting in improved stability and generalization. Additionally, a collaborative learning mechanism is introduced to enable adaptive interaction among model components, further enhancing robustness in complex and noisy environments. The proposed framework also leverages kernel-induced feature mapping to capture nonlinear relationships without requiring explicit hidden-layer selection, maintaining both efficiency and scalability. Extensive experiments on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets demonstrate that KRPRVFL consistently outperforms baseline models in terms of accuracy, robustness, and statistical significance, highlighting its effectiveness as a fast, scalable, and reliable solution for challenging classification tasks.

</details>


### [56] [Contrastive Learning for Interpretable Anomaly Detection at Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13652)
*Haoyi Jia,Sagar Addepalli,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ORCA两阶段框架，通过监督对比学习将多种物理过程映射到嵌入空间，再利用自编码器在该空间中生成事件级异常分数，解决高能对撞机物理中异常检测分数难以解释以及分数与能量尺度和事例多重性强相关的问题。在HL-LHC模拟数据上，ORCA相比基线自编码器在敏感性的广度和深度上均有显著提升，且嵌入空间使得异常样本具有可解释性，可通过最大似然模板拟合准确恢复注入信号产额并量化不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能对撞机物理的通用事件级异常检测存在两个反复出现的问题：异常分数难以解释，且分数与能标和事例多重性强相关。现有方法缺乏对异常事件物理含义的洞察，限制了其在搜寻新物理中的应用价值。

Method: ORCA采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过监督对比学习在多样化的物理过程集合上学习嵌入空间；第二阶段在该嵌入空间中运行标准自编码器生成事件级异常分数。此外，利用嵌入空间中已知过程占据不同区域的性质，通过最大似然模板拟合对嵌入分布进行拟合，归因异常样本并量化不确定性。

Result: 在符合HL-LHC条件的模拟数据集上，ORCA相比基线自编码器在对新物理信号的敏感性广度和深度上均获得显著增益。最大似然模板拟合能准确恢复注入信号产额（包括未参与嵌入训练的信号），并能通过已知过程描述模板库中缺失信号的特征。相比标准一维输出拟合，嵌入几何承载更高维物理信息，增强了后续统计分析能力。

Conclusion: ORCA确立为对撞机上可实现可解释异常检测搜寻的路径，其嵌入几何携带更高维度的物理信息，优于标准一维输出拟合，为下游统计分析提供增强支持。

Abstract: Generic event-level anomaly detection for collider physics has two recurring problems: anomaly scores are hard to interpret, and they correlate strongly with energy scale and object multiplicity. We present Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection (ORCA), a two-stage framework that first learns an embedding space via supervised contrastive learning across a diverse set of physics processes, then runs a standard autoencoder in that space to generate event-level anomaly scores. On a simulated dataset consistent with conditions at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, ORCA delivers significant gains in both breadth and depth of sensitivity to new physics signals with respect to a baseline autoencoder architecture. Beyond improved sensitivity, the contrastive embedding makes the anomalous sample interpretable: because known processes occupy distinct regions of the space, a maximum-likelihood template fit to the embedding distributions can attribute events in an anomalous sample to template physics processes with quantified uncertainties. We demonstrate that the fit accurately recovers injected signal yields, including for signals excluded from the training of the embedding, and characterizes signals absent from the template library through the known processes they most resemble. These results establish ORCA as a route to interpretable anomaly detection-based searches at colliders, where the embedding geometry carries higher dimensional physics information compared to standard one-dimensional output fits, enhancing downstream statistical analysis.

</details>


### [57] [The Query Knows What to Forget: A Second Erase Direction for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13668)
*Dhruman Gupta,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Query-derived Erase Direction (QED)，在Gated DeltaNet-2中从查询向量推导第二个、与键正交的擦除方向，解决线性注意力在长上下文下因状态干扰导致的检索退化问题，使可用上下文长度约翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力保持固定大小的状态，长上下文下多个存储条目共享该状态，相互干扰导致检索退化。GDN-2等所有delta-rule模型都是从当前token的键推导擦除向量，但读取中的干扰是通过查询测量的，擦除步骤无法触及查询方向上的干扰，存在结构性错配。

Method: 提出QED：在原有键推导的擦除方向之外，增加一个从查询推导、与键正交的第二擦除方向。在快速权重视角下，键方向的delta编辑无法修改读取中与键正交的部分，QED利用可编辑部分来抵消沿查询方向测量的旧状态内容。

Result: QED在训练窗口之外的每个长度上都改善了检索能力，在S-NIAH-1任务上使可用上下文长度约翻倍。

Conclusion: 通过引入查询推导的正交擦除方向，QED有效解决了delta-rule模型中擦除与读取方向不匹配的问题，显著提升了线性注意力在长上下文下的检索性能。

Abstract: Linear attention keeps a state of fixed size. At long context, many stored items share this state, and interference between them degrades retrieval. Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2), like every delta-rule model before it, derives its erase vector from the key of the current token. However, the interference in its reads is measured through the query, and the erase step cannot reach it. We introduce the Query-derived Erase Direction (QED). QED adds a second erase direction derived from the query and orthogonal to the key. In the fast-weight view, a key-directed delta edit cannot change the key-orthogonal part of a read. It uses the editable part to cancel old-state content measured along the query. It also improves retrieval at every length past the training window, and it about doubles the usable context length on S-NIAH-1.

</details>


### [58] [From BERT to Frontier Agents: Eight Years of Language-Model Progress, the Collapse of the Capability-Cost Curve, and the Rise of Task-Targeted Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13675)
*Pranav Kumar Kaliaperumal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文概述了2018年10月至2026年7月间AI模型从BERT等简单系统演进为能解决复杂数学和编写软件的大型智能体的历程。AI解决真实编码问题的能力自2024年底以来每年提升近6倍，同时成本大幅下降（OpenAI的GPT-5.6 Luna以每百万token 1-6美元的价格匹配旗舰能力）。顶级性能分散在多个专业模型中：Claude Opus 5在前端编码领先，Claude Fable 5擅长仓库级编码，GPT-5.6 Sol在终端任务中表现优异。在小学数学测试中，基础方法解决58/100题，高级采样方法解决79题；置信度排序工具在前50个选项中正确识别47个正确答案，所有研究材料已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追踪和评估AI模型在大时间跨度（2018-2026）内的能力演进轨迹，量化其在编码、数学等领域的能力提升速度，同时评估成本效益变化，以揭示当前AI生态系统中各专业模型的技术定位与实用价值。

Method: 采用多维度评估方法：跟踪AI解决真实编码问题的能力指标并计算能力提升速率；对比不同模型及其定价策略下的性价比；在年级数学测试中对比基础采样与高级采样方法的解题率；构建置信度排名工具并评估其Top-50选取中的准确率。

Result: AI解决真实编码问题能力自2024年底以来每年提升约6倍；GPT-5.6 Luna以极低成本（每百万token 1-6美元）匹配旗舰能力；专业模型分化显著（Claude Opus 5前端编码、Claude Fable 5仓库级编码、GPT-5.6 Sol终端任务）；高级采样在数学测试中达79%解题率（对比基础方法58%）；置信度工具Top-50中正确识别47个答案。

Conclusion: AI模型在短短数年内实现了数量级的能力跃升和成本下降，当前技术格局已呈现专业化分工趋势。高级采样与置信度排序等工程方法能显著提升实际任务表现，所有研究材料均已公开，为该领域的后续研究提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Between October 2018 and July 2026 AI models progressed from simple systems like BERT to massive agents that solve complex math and write software. The ability to resolve real coding issues improved by nearly six times per year since late 2024. During this time costs dropped sharply with OpenAIs budget model GPT 5 point 6 Luna matching flagship capabilities for just one to six dollars per million tokens beating older versions at a fraction of the price. Top performance is now split across specialized models as Claude Opus 5 leads in frontend coding Claude Fable 5 excels at repository level coding and GPT 5 point 6 Sol dominates terminal tasks. In a grade school math test using the Qwen 2 point 5 model basic methods solved 58 of 100 problems while advanced sampling solved up to 79. A confidence ranking tool correctly identified 47 right answers in its top 50 choices proving highly useful for sorting tasks with all research materials made fully public.

</details>


### [59] [High-dimensional nonparametric changepoint detection via low-rank degree-two density projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13922)
*Guoqing Zhang,Zhaixin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于表示（representation-based）的高维分布变化检测方法，通过构造对称特征矩阵H_2(X)保留至多二阶的密度信息，将密度估计转化为矩阵均值估计，并利用秩r截断后的矩阵CUSUM进行扫描。该方法在非参数设定下实现低秩分布跳变的检测，具有桶形（tent-shaped）总体目标函数和最优的非渐近算子范数界，支持多变点检测，并达到Le Cam下界意义下的最优性（至多差对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维场景下，当变化前后的密度均无参数化形式时，检测分布变化极为困难。现有方法往往依赖稀疏性假设或参数模型，无法有效处理纯依赖结构变化（如相关性变化）这类对均值不可见的变化。作者希望构建一种无需密度估计、不依赖稀疏性假设的方法，能够捕获低秩的分布跳变信息。

Method: 1) 构造特征矩阵H_2(X)∈R^{(d+1)×(d+1)}，使得M(f)=E_f H_2(X)是密度二阶正交投影的等距编码，从而用矩阵均值估计替代密度估计；2) 对特征矩阵均值进行秩r截断后扫描矩阵CUSUM，利用投影跳变的低秩结构而非坐标稀疏性；3) 设计了种子化最窄超过阈值（seeded narrowest-over-threshold）程序处理多变点，并通过保持隔离区间的归纳法证明精确恢复；4) 引入交叉拟合标量细化（cross-fitted scalar refinement），在一折学习低秩变化方向、在另一折定位变化点；5) 扩展到几何β-混合依赖数据。

Result: 1) 领先随机项以O(√(rd log(nd)))缩放，具有非渐近算子范数分析；2) 交叉拟合细化方法达到Õ_p(κ^{-2})定位误差，并通过匹配的Le Cam下界证明其在对数因子意义下的最优性；3) 实验验证：环境维度高达200、含三个变化的d=100序列、128特征的HAR（人体活动识别）基准，方法计算高效且能准确检测均值CUSUM无法察觉的纯依赖结构变化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一套在非参数高维设定下检测分布变化（特别是纯依赖结构变化）的完整理论与算法框架：通过特征矩阵编码低秩投影跳变，兼顾了计算可行性、非渐近理论保证、多处变化恢复以及依赖数据的扩展，并在Le Cam下界意义上证明了定位精度的最优性。

Abstract: Detecting distributional changes in high dimension is difficult when neither the pre-change nor post-change density is parametrically specified. We introduce a representation-based approach that retains all degree-at-most-two density information while replacing density estimation by matrix mean estimation. For observations in $[-1,1]^d$, a symmetric feature matrix $H_2(X)\in\R^{(d+1)\times(d+1)}$ is constructed so that $M(f)=\E_f H_2(X)$ is an isometric encoding of the degree-two orthogonal projection of the density. We scan matrix CUSUMs after rank-$r$ truncation, exploiting the low rank of the projected jump rather than sparsity of individual coordinates. The resulting \LRD{} estimator has a tent-shaped population objective and a nonasymptotic operator-norm analysis whose leading stochastic term scales as $\sqrt{rd\log(nd)}$. For multiple changes, we give a seeded narrowest-over-threshold procedure and prove exact recovery by an induction that preserves an isolating interval for every undetected change. A cross-fitted scalar refinement learns the changing low-rank direction on one fold and localizes on the other, attaining $\widetilde O_{\Pp}(κ^{-2})$ error; a matching Le Cam lower bound shows optimality up to logarithms. A geometrically $β$-mixing extension follows from a dependent matrix Bernstein inequality. Experiments with ambient dimension up to $200$, a three-change $d=100$ sequence, and a $128$-feature human-activity benchmark show that the method remains computationally practical and accurately detects pure dependence changes that are invisible to mean CUSUMs.

</details>


### [60] [EEG-PRISM: Physiologically-Grounded Interpretability of Predictions by EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13676)
*Deeksha M Shama,Punnisa Amornsirikul,Archana Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EEG-PRISM是一种通用的后验归因方法，通过线性变换和反向传播规则将EEG基础模型的时间-通道归因分数映射到频率域和源域，实现生理学相关的可解释性，无需修改或重新训练底层模型。在癫痫和自闭症数据上分别实现了频谱恢复和病灶定位/生物标志物识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型是EEG分析中AI的下一个进展，但现有可解释AI技术仅在时间-通道输入空间提供归因分数，与临床对EEG的直觉理解不匹配。需要一个通用方法，能将任意基础模型的可解释性扩展到替代且生理相关的域中。

Method: EEG-PRISM利用线性变换和已建立的反向传播规则，将时间-通道归因分数映射到替代域。通过可逆DFT映射到频率域，通过近似可逆的EEG生成模型映射到源域。在模拟数据和真实数据上使用五个基础模型和四个AI解释器评估跨域的地面真实现象恢复能力。

Result: 模拟实验中实现近完美的频谱恢复和69.2%的空间准确率。癫痫数据中正确判定delta-theta活动最显著，并以50%准确率正确定位癫痫发作起始区域。自闭症数据中将预测性的delta-alpha生物标志物定位到额叶和颞叶区域，与先前研究一致。

Conclusion: EEG-PRISM是一种理论上有据可依的后验归因方法，能在频谱域和空间域实现准确的映射。它支持瞬态事件（如癫痫发作）的窗口级分析和临床相关生物标志物（如自闭症）的群体级识别，推进了可解释EEG基础模型的发展。

Abstract: Objective: Foundation models represent the next advancement in AI for EEG analysis; however current explainable AI techniques provide attribution scores in the time-channel input space, which is mismatched to clinical intuition about EEG. Thus, there is a critical need for a universal method that can extend the interpretability of any foundation model to alternative and physiologically relevant domains without modifying or retraining the underlying model. Methods: EEG-PRISM leverages linear transformations and established backpropagation rules to map time-channel attribution scores into alternative domains. We derive mappings to the frequency domain via an invertible DFT and to the source domain via an approximately invertible EEG generative model. We evaluate EEG-PRISM in simulated and real data, assessing recovery of ground-truth phenomena across domains with five foundation models and four AI explainers. Results: In simulation, EEG-PRISM achieves near-perfect spectral recovery and 69.2% spatial accuracy. In epilepsy, EEG-PRISM correctly determines that delta-theta activity is most salient and correctly localizes the seizure onset region with 50% accuracy. In autism, EEG-PRISM localizes the predictive delta-alpha biomarkers to frontal and temporal regions, consistent with prior work. Conclusion: EEG-PRISM is a theoretically-grounded post-hoc attribution method with accurate mapping into the spectral and spatial domains. It supports window-level analysis of transient events (e.g., seizures) and group-level identification of clinically relevant biomarkers (e.g., autism), thus advancing interpretable EEG foundation models. Significance: This work enables physiologically-grounded interpretation of EEG foundation models and supports clinically relevant insights such as event localization and biomarker identification.

</details>


### [61] [QUASAR: Lowering the Loss Floor of Quantization-Aware Training with Loss-Aware Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13966)
*Vincent Counathe,Ben Athiwaratkun,Christopher De Sa,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUASAR是一种新颖的量化感知训练（QAT）方法，通过在训练循环中持续进行轻量级的损失感知重建来降低损失下限并提升低比特量化模型质量。每个训练步骤使用指数移动平均的平方梯度作为在线显著性估计，搜索少量裁剪范围，并通过显著性加权最小二乘拟合仿射反量化器。在Qwen3和Llama-3.1上，QUASAR在2、3、4比特设置下取得最低的KL散度，相比基线减少至少10%（3/4比特）和29%（2比特），2比特下平均准确率提升3.5-4.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
