# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Algebraic curves admitting automorphism groups of large prime square order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13768)
*Nazar Arakelian,Diego Kian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了代数闭域（任意特征）上具有素数阶平方（ℓ²）自同构群的曲线，给出了素数 ℓ 大小的界，并分类了在驯服情形和野性情形下达到上界的曲线族，最后给出了达到最高界的曲线的完整自同构群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意特征的代数闭域上，理解曲线自同构群的结构是代数几何中的基础问题。对于阶为素数平方 ℓ² 的自同构群，需要确定 ℓ 可以达到的最大值，并理解达到极值的曲线族及其完整自同构群，这有助于深入刻画曲线的对称性。

Method: 本文采用代数几何中曲线自同构理论的方法，分别处理驯服情形（ℓ 不等于域特征）和野性情形（ℓ 等于域特征）。通过分析自同构群的阶为 ℓ² 时的曲线亏格与 ℓ 的关系，建立界，并对达到上界的曲线族进行分类，最后计算这些曲线的完整自同构群。

Result: 给出了素数 ℓ 大小的下界/上界（具体界值需见全文），在驯服和野性两种情形下分类了达到 ℓ 上界的曲线族，并呈现了达到最高界的曲线的完整自同构群。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了阶为 ℓ² 的自同构群中素数 ℓ 的界限问题，给出了两种情形下达到极值的曲线族的完整分类及其全自同构群，为曲线对称性理论提供了新的结果。

Abstract: Let $\mathbb{K}$ denote an algebraically closed field of arbitrary characteristic. In this paper we provide bounds for the size of a prime $\ell$ for which there are curves defined over $\mathbb{K}$ admitting automorphism groups of order $\ell^2$. In addition, we also give a classification of the families of curves attaining the upper bounds for $\ell$ in both tame and wild case. Finally, we present the full automorphism groups of the curves attaining the highest bounds.

</details>


### [122] [The Lie algebra of polynomial vector fields on the affine space with constant divergence is $1.5$-generated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13798)
*Ivan Beldiev,Gleb Pogudin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明仿射空间上具有常散度的多项式向量场构成的李代数是1.5-生成的，并证明复仿射空间上所有多项式向量场构成的李代数可由两个完全可积元素生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究李代数的生成性质，特别是1.5-生成这一较强的最小生成性质，以及探究多项式向量场李代数中完全可积元素的生成能力。

Method: 通过构建每个非零元素都能补全为二元生成集的显式构造方法，证明常散度多项式向量场李代数的1.5-生成性；同时构造两个完全可积元素来生成整个多项式向量场李代数。

Result: 证明了常散度多项式向量场李代数是1.5-生成的，且复仿射空间上全体多项式向量场李代数可由两个完全可积元素生成。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了多项式向量场李代数在生成性方面具有非常强的性质，特别是1.5-生成性和完全可积元素生成的结果，深化了对这类李代数结构性质的理解。

Abstract: A Lie algebra is said to be $1.5$-generated if every nonzero element can be completed to a two-element generating set. We prove that the Lie algebra of polynomial vector fields with constant divergence on the affine space is $1.5$-generated. We also show that the Lie algebra of all polynomial vector fields on the complex affine space is generated by two completely integrable elements.

</details>


### [123] [Twisted forms of classical hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13896)
*Alvaro Liendo,Matías Vásquez*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在实数域和有限域上，对三大类具有大自同构群的光滑超曲面（Fermat、Delsarte和Klein超曲面）的扭曲形式进行计数。主要工具是一个关于Galois上同调集的计数公式，适用于自同构群为对角交换群与置换群的半直积的光滑超曲面。为在有限域上应用该公式，作者将Oguiso-Yu的微分方法扩展到正特征，并在p的显式算术条件下计算正特征代数闭域上这三类超曲面的自同构群。在实数域上的计数恢复了Sasaki关于Fermat超曲面实形式的最新定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭曲形式（twisted forms）的分类与计数是算术几何中的基本问题。已有工作处理了Fermat超曲面在实数域上的实形式（如Sasaki的定理），但缺乏一个统一的、系统的方法来同时处理实数域和有限域上三类经典超曲面的扭曲形式计数问题，尤其在正特征情形缺乏系统的自同构群计算结果。

Method: 1) 建立计数公式：对自同构群为对角阿贝尔群与单项置换群的半直积的光滑超曲面，给出Galois上同调集H^1(Gal, Aut(X))的计数公式。2) 将Oguiso-Yu的微分方法推广到正特征，使得能够在有限域上应用该计数公式。3) 在p满足显式算术条件时，计算正特征代数闭域上Fermat、Delsarte和Klein超曲面的自同构群。

Result: 在实数域和有限域上分别得到了Fermat、Delsarte和Klein三类光滑超曲面的扭曲形式计数。在实数域上的计数结果恢复了Sasaki关于Fermat超曲面实形式的最新定理，表明该方法的正确性和普适性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的框架来计数几类经典超曲面的扭曲形式，同时覆盖了实数域和有限域两种情形，并将微分几何方法成功推广至正特征。这为更一般类别的超曲面扭曲形式研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We count the twisted forms, over the field of real numbers and over finite fields, of the three classical families of smooth hypersurfaces with large automorphism group: the Fermat, Delsarte and Klein hypersurfaces. Our main tool is a counting formula for the Galois cohomology set of a smooth hypersurface whose automorphism group is the semidirect product of a diagonal abelian group and a group of permutations of the monomials of its defining equation. In order to apply this formula over finite fields, we extend the differential method of Oguiso and Yu to positive characteristic, and we compute the automorphism groups of the Fermat, Delsarte and Klein hypersurfaces over algebraically closed fields of positive characteristic under explicit arithmetic conditions on p. Over the reals, our count recovers a recent theorem of Sasaki on the real forms of Fermat hypersurfaces.

</details>


### [124] [Rank-two weak Fano bundles on a four-dimensional quadric hypersurface $Q^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13916)
*Yuta Takahashi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文对四维光滑二次超曲面Q^4上的秩2弱Fano丛进行了完全分类。结果表明，在张量线丛的意义下，这类丛仅有三种类型：分裂丛、旋量丛，或[APW94]中构造的具有陈类c1=-1和c2=(1,1)的稳定丛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对弱Fano丛进行分类是代数几何中的一个基本问题。四维二次超曲面上的秩2向量丛分类此前尚未完成，该论文填补了这一空白，并建立了与已有构造（[APW94]）之间的联系。

Method: 论文通过对秩2弱Fano丛在不同陈类情况下的结构分析，结合二次超曲面的几何性质，逐一排除或识别可能的丛类型，最终将其归约为三种标准情形：分裂丛、旋量丛和[APW94]中的稳定丛。

Result: 证明了任何秩2弱Fano丛在张量线丛后，必为分裂丛、旋量丛，或[APW94]中陈类为c1=-1, c2=(1,1)的稳定丛之一。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了四维二次超曲面上秩2弱Fano丛的完整分类，深化了对高维二次超曲面上一族向量丛结构的理解。

Abstract: We classify rank $2$ weak Fano bundles on a four-dimensional smooth quadric hypersurface $Q^4$. Up to twisting with a line bundle, such a bundle is either a split bundle, a spinor bundle, or one of the stable bundles with Chern classes $c_1=-1$ and $c_2=(1,1)$ constructed in [APW94].

</details>


### [125] [Scrollar invariants of singular curves on toric surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14080)
*Karl Christ,Xiang He,Ilya Tyomkin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究环面曲面上一般整曲线在单项式投影下对应的滚动不变量（scrollar invariants）。对于一大类环面曲面，确定了固定几何亏格的一般整曲线的滚动不变量，推广了Castryck和Cools关于光滑曲线（特征零）的组合公式。同时描述了任意环面曲面的预期行为，并给出在某些Severi簇不可约分量上该预期行为失败的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滚动不变量的研究源于曲线到射影线的映射，是理解曲线几何的重要工具。此前Castryck和Cools只处理了特征零下的光滑曲线情形，本文旨在将其推广到更一般的整曲线（可能奇异）以及更广泛的环面曲面类别，并在正特征或奇异情形下探索该公式的适用范围与局限性。

Method: 从环面曲面上的曲线出发，利用单项式投影诱导从曲线正规化到射影线的映射。通过对固定几何亏格的一般整曲线进行分析，建立组合公式来计算滚动不变量，并考察其在Severi簇不同不可约分量上的行为。

Result: 对于一大类环面曲面，确定了固定几何亏格的一般整曲线的滚动不变量，成功推广了Castryck和Cools的组合公式。同时构造了反例，表明在一般情形下该公式至少在某些Severi簇的不可约分量上失效。

Conclusion: 该工作将光滑曲线情形的组合公式推广到了一般整曲线，揭示了滚动不变量计算的更普适规律，同时也指出这一规律并非对任意环面曲面普遍适用，存在例外情形，为后续进一步研究指明了边界条件。

Abstract: Given a curve on a toric surface, a monomial projection induces a map from the normalization of the curve to the projective line. We determine the associated scrollar invariants for general integral curves of fixed geometric genus for a large class of toric surfaces. This generalizes a combinatorial formula to calculate such scrollar invariants for smooth curves in characteristic zero due to Castryck and Cools. We describe an expected behaviour for any toric surface, but provide examples where this fails at least on some irreducible component of the corresponding Severi variety.

</details>


### [126] [The Hassett-Keel program via $Θ$-stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14099)
*Luca Tasin,Filippo Viviani*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Beyond GIT方法中曲线α-稳定性的内在概念，证明其可以恢复9/11<α≤1时的Deligne-Mumford稳定性，并且α-半稳定轨道位于Hassett-Keel计划的已知模紧化中，适用于α>2/3-ε。作为应用，还获得了光滑曲线的新的α-稳定性结果。方法结合了斜率不等式与退化定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在从Beyond GIT的视角出发，建立α-稳定性与经典模空间稳定性（如Deligne-Mumford稳定性）之间的联系，从而推进Hassett-Keel计划中的模空间紧化研究，并为光滑曲线提供新的稳定性判据。

Method: 作者采用两种主要工具：一是斜率不等式（slope inequalities），用于分析曲线的稳定性条件；二是退化定理（degeneration theorem），该定理表明任何具有非节点奇点的Gorenstein曲线都可以等变退化（isotrivially degenerated）为具有G_m作用的Gorenstein曲线，从而简化稳定性分析。

Result: 主要结果表明：当9/11<α≤1时，α-稳定性等价于Deligne-Mumford稳定性；当α>2/3-ε时，α-半稳定轨道包含在已知的Hassett-Keel模紧化中。此外，作为推论，论文还得到了光滑曲线的新α-稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了α-稳定性在特定参数范围内与经典模空间稳定性一致，并为Hassett-Keel计划提供了新的理论支撑，同时也为Future研究Gorenstein曲线的模紧化问题提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We study the intrinsic notion of $α$-stability for curves arising from the Beyond GIT approach to moduli spaces. We show that it recovers Deligne-Mumford stability for $9/11<α\le1$, and more generally that the $α$-semistable locus is contained in the known modular compactification of the Hassett-Keel proram for $α>2/3-\varepsilon$. As applications, we also obtain new $α$-stability results for smooth curves. Our approach combines slope inequalities with a degeneration theorem showing that every Gorenstein curve with a non-nodal singularity can be isotrivially degenerated to a Gorenstein curve with a $\mathbb G_m$-action.

</details>


### [127] [The alpha spectrum of K-polystable toric $\mathbb{Q}$-Fano varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14115)
*Xian Wu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文完全确定了n维K-多重稳定环面Q-Fano簇的普通（非等变）全局不变量的数值谱，证明其恰好为有理数集与[1/(n+1), 1/2]区间的交集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在强化Liu和Zhuang提出的问题，并精炼Liu和Zhu最近的构造结果，完整刻画K-多重稳定环面Q-Fano簇的全局不变量α(X)所能取到的所有数值。

Method: 通过构造具体的K-多重稳定环面Q-Fano簇的例子，证明其普通全局不变量的数值可以覆盖区间[1/(n+1), 1/2]中的所有有理数，从而建立完整的环面实现定理。

Result: 证明了α(X)的取值集合恰好等于Q∩[1/(n+1), 1/2]，即建立了完整的环面实现定理，同时给出了上下界并说明了可达性。

Conclusion: 该结果完全解决了Liu-Zhuang问题的一个"环面情形"，将之前的构造性结果提升为完整的数值刻画定理；值得注意的是，作者提及初始构造思路来自GPT-5.6 Sol的建议。

Abstract: We explicitly determine the numerical spectrum of the ordinary, non-equivariant global invariant for $n$-dimensional K-polystable toric $\mathbb{Q}$-Fano varieties. Specifically, we prove that $$\left\{ α(X)\mid X\text{ is an $n$-dimensional K-polystable toric $\mathbb{Q}$-Fano variety}\right \} = \mathbb{Q}\cap \left[\frac{1}{n+1},\frac{1}{2}\right].$$ This result establishes a complete toric realization theorem, which strengthens a question raised by Liu and Zhuang and refines the recent construction results of Liu and Zhu. The initial construction of the examples in this work was suggested by GPT-5.6 Sol, and was subsequently refined and rigorously developed by the author.

</details>


### [128] [The Quartic Hessian Conjecture in Dimension Four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14217)
*Zixiang Ni*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了四元四次情形下的Hessian猜想：任何具有非零常数Hessian行列式的四元四次多项式均具有多项式梯度逆。证明将四次情形系统地分解为三种类型（真三元四次、真二元四次、线性形式的四次幂），每种类型均约化到形如 f=P(x1,x2,x3)+x4Q(x1,x2,x3)+a·x4² 的标准形式，再独立于P的次数与顶部部分证明该类多项式的梯度可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hessian猜想是代数几何与多项式映射理论中的著名开放问题。该猜想在维数≤3时已成立，在维数≥5时已被证伪，唯独四维情形长期悬而未决。本文聚焦四维猜想中最基本的四次情形，旨在推进四维Hessian猜想的证明进程。

Method: 证明分为两大步骤：(1) 分类约化——利用四维齐次Hesse定理，将顶部齐次部分（Hessian行列式为零）的锥代表分为三类：真三元四次（非零三元Hessian）、真二元四次和线性形式的四次幂；分别利用七次、六次和五次行列式方程及常数方向引理，将所有情形约化到标准形式 f=P(x1,x2,x3)+x4Q(x1,x2,x3)+a·x4²（deg Q≤2）。(2) 标准形式可逆性证明——独立于P的度数与顶部部分：a≠0分支通过Schur补从已知的三维Hessian猜想导出；a=0分支通过各向同性锥的秩分析排除秩2情形，用显式三角逆解决秩1例外，并将秩0约化到二维Hessian猜想。

Result: 成功证明了四元四次情形的Hessian猜想成立。所有可能的常数Hessian四元四次多项式均被证明具有多项式梯度逆。

Conclusion: 通过系统地将四元四次情形归约到三种代数类型并逐一攻克，本文首次完整解决了四维Hessian猜想在四次情形下的实例，为最终攻克四维一般情形的Hessian猜想提供了重要的方法学基础。

Abstract: The Hessian conjecture asks whether a polynomial with nonzero constant Hessian determinant has a polynomial gradient inverse. It is known in dimensions at most three, false in dimensions at least five, and open in dimension four. We prove its four-variable quartic case.
  The top homogeneous part has zero Hessian determinant and, by the four-dimensional homogeneous Hesse theorem, is a cone. We divide its cone representative into three exhaustive types: a genuinely ternary quartic with nonzero ternary Hessian, a genuinely binary quartic, and a fourth power of a linear form. In the first type, the degree-seven determinant equation forces the cubic part to be affine-linear in the cone direction. In the binary type, the degree-six equation gives a constant null direction in a two-variable Hessian of the cubic part. In the unary type, the degree-five equation and a constant-direction lemma give the same conclusion. Every type therefore reduces to \[ f=P(x_1,x_2,x_3)+x_4Q(x_1,x_2,x_3)+a x_4^2, \qquad °Q\leq2. \] We prove, independently of the degree or top part of \(P\), that every constant-Hessian polynomial of this form has a polynomial gradient inverse. The branch \(a\ne0\) descends from the known three-dimensional Hessian conjecture after a Schur complement. When \(a=0\), an isotropic-cone rank analysis eliminates rank two, solves the rank-one exception by an explicit triangular inverse, and reduces rank zero to the two-dimensional Hessian conjecture. The coupled degree-six identity is retained throughout; no component with respect to a fixed quadratic form is separated.

</details>


### [129] [The cone theorem for effective fourfold pairs in characteristic $p>5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14236)
*Joe Waldron*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在假设对数分辨率猜想对所有与X双有理的对数对成立的前提下，证明了正特征和混合特征p>5的射影对数典范、Q-因子四维对数对(X,Δ)在K_X+Δ≡M≥0条件下的锥定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正特征和混合特征下建立极小模型纲领中的锥定理是一个长期未解决的问题。本文旨在将特征正/混合情形下四维对数对的锥定理证明推进，特别是在p>5的情形下，为完整建立正特征MMP奠定基础。

Method: 本文采用假设对数分辨率猜想成立作为前提条件，通过对射影对数典范四维对数对进行结构分析，利用K_X+Δ同余于一个有效除子M的非负性条件，在特征p>5的正特征和混合特征基上推导锥定理的证明。

Result: 在正特征和混合特征p>5的情形下，证明了射影对数典范、Q-因子四维对数对的锥定理，其中要求K_X+Δ≡M≥0。该结果的成立依赖于对数分辨率猜想（对所考虑的对数对双有理等价类）的假设。

Conclusion: 该工作将锥定理的证明从特征零推广到了正特征和混合特征p>5的四维情形，条件性地建立在对数分辨率猜想之上。这一结果是对正特征极小模型纲领的重要推进，为后续在正特征下建立完整的MMP提供了关键的理论支撑。

Abstract: Assuming the log resolution conjecture for all log pairs birational to $X$, we prove the cone theorem for projective log canonical, $\mathbb{Q}$-factorial fourfold pairs $(X,Δ)$ such that $K_X +Δ\equiv M \geq 0$ over bases of positive and mixed characteristic $p > 5$.

</details>


### [130] [Estimation of calculation errors and resolution of singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14271)
*Victor Fadinger-Held,Daniel Windisch*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇阐述性文章，引入奇点消解和实对数规范阈值（RLCT）作为衡量数学错误“有多严重”的工具，并以高中常见的数学错误（如“新生之梦”（a+b)²=a²+b²）为例计算该不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，我们只是简单地将数学错误判定为“完全错误”，但缺乏一个定量的方法来衡量错误到底有多严重。作者希望利用与最小模型纲领（MMP）及贝叶斯统计、机器学习紧密相关的RLCT作为度量指标，为数学错误提供更精细的量化分析。

Method: 作者采用解析与代数几何的工具——奇点消解和实对数规范阈值（RLCT）——来计算各类错误对应的不变量。对多个典型错误类别（如“新生之梦”展开式错误、基础统计中均值计算的错误）进行RLCT的计算，并展示如何使用计算机代数软件辅助求解。

Result: 作者为若干高中水平的典型数学错误精确计算了RLCT值，展示了这一不变量作为错误严重性度量的可行性，并演示了计算机代数软件在该计算过程中的应用。

Conclusion: RLCT为数学错误提供了一种新颖且定量的描述方式，将原本简单的“对/错”二元判断拓展为更有信息量的度量，同时也展示了奇点理论、代数几何与统计学之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: There are two possible answers to "$(a+b)^2 = a^2 + b^2$". Either "this is completely wrong" or "I assume, you are in characteristic $2$". If we go for the first answer, we could ask ourselves: "How wrong is it actually?" In this expository article, we introduce and use resolution of singularities and the real log canonical threshold (RLCT) as a measure for such errors. The RLCT is an invariant with connections to the minimal model program and, as recently discovered and investigated, to Bayesian statistics and machine learning. Inspired by typical mistakes from high school mathematics, we compute this invariant for several classes of examples, among them the error arising from the "freshman's dream" above and from a flawed computation of means in elementary statistics. In some examples, we highlight how a computer algebra software can be used in order to solve this problem.

</details>


### [131] [Weight decomposition for toroidal abelian fibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14337)
*Younghan Bae,Jeremy Feusi,Aitor Iribar Lopez,Sam Molcho*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有理乘N映射在环秩至多为一的主极化阿贝尔簇退化簇的Chow群上的作用，及其与傅里叶变换的交互关系，并由此计算单位截面的类、证明相对Chow motive的广义权分解，并完全刻画其万有族的tautological环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解退化情形下主极化阿贝尔簇的Chow群结构及其上的有理自同态（乘N映射）作用，是算术几何和代数几何中的核心问题。此前的工作已对光滑情形有深入理解，但退化情形（环秩至多为一）的相应结果尚不完善，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用有理乘N映射在退化主极化阿贝尔簇Chow群上的作用，结合傅里叶变换的交互性质，通过分析单位截面的类、相对Chow motive的权分解以及tautological环的结构来完成研究。

Result: 计算了单位截面的类，证明了广义权分解下的相对Chow motive结构，并完全确定了退化情形下万有族的tautological环。

Conclusion: 本文为环秩至多为一的主极化阿贝尔簇退化范畴建立了完整的Chow理论框架，统一了乘N映射与傅里叶变换的相互作用，为后续研究更高环秩退化情形奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: We study the action of the rational multiplication by $N$ map on the Chow groups of degenerations of principally polarized abelian varieties of torus rank at most one, as well as its interaction with the Fourier transform. As applications, we compute the class of the unit section, prove a generalized weight decomposition of the relative Chow motive of the universal family of such degenerations, and completely determine its tautological ring.

</details>


### [132] [Semi-abelian reduction of Albanese varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14417)
*Tai-Hsuan Chung*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于阿贝尔簇半阿贝尔约化的定理：若射影几何正规簇的局部域上的特殊纤维在余维数一处至多具有节点，则其阿尔巴尼斯簇具有半阿贝尔约化。据此推广了Fontaine和Abrashkin关于Z上阿贝尔概形不存在的经典定理到奇异情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Fontaine-Abrashkin定理断言在整数环Z上不存在非平凡的阿贝尔概形，这一结果在算术几何中具有重要意义。然而该定理仅限于光滑情形（阿贝尔概形），作者希望将其推广到包含奇点的更一般的射影概形设定中，从而理解奇异算术对象的内在性质。

Method: 作者考虑一个DVR的分数域K上的射影几何正规簇X_K，假设其存在一个射影模型使得特殊纤维在余维数一处至多具有节点（即最坏情况的奇点仅出现在codimension one），然后研究其阿尔巴尼斯簇Alb_{X_K/K}的约化性质。通过分析阿尔巴尼斯映射与特殊纤维的节点奇异性之间的关系，证明阿尔巴尼斯簇具有半阿贝尔约化，进而应用该结果结合Fontaine的经典方法处理Z上的奇异情形。

Result: 证明了对具有至多余维数一处节点奇点的射影模型，其阿尔巴尼斯簇具有半阿贝尔约化。作为应用，将Fontaine-Abrashkin不存在性定理推广到奇异情形：若Z上的平坦射影概形X具有正规且几何连通的纤维，则H^1(X_Q, O_{X_Q})=0，这意味着不存在满足上述条件的非平凡几何例子。

Conclusion: 本文确立了奇异算术簇的阿尔巴尼斯簇与半阿贝尔约化之间的内在联系，并将经典的整环上阿贝尔概形不存在性定理推广至更一般的奇异射影簇，深化了对整数环上射影几何对象的约束理解。

Abstract: Let X_K be a projective geometrically normal variety over the fraction field of a DVR. We show that if X_K admits a projective model whose special fibre has at worst nodes in codimension one, then the Albanese variety Alb_{X_K/K} has semi-abelian reduction over the same base. As an application, we extend the classical nonexistence theorem of Fontaine and Abrashkin for abelian schemes over Z to the singular setting. For instance, if a flat projective Z-scheme X has normal and geometrically connected fibres, then H^1(X_Q, O_{X_Q})=0.

</details>


### [133] [Classifying Smooth Quot Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14424)
*Roy Skjelnes,Gregory G. Smith,Michael Stillman*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究$\mathbb{P}^n$上秩$r$平凡向量丛的Quot scheme的几何性质，确定了使Quot scheme光滑且不可约的Hilbert多项式$q$的完全数值条件，并发现了lexicographic点的光滑性等更多几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Quot scheme是代数几何中的基本研究对象，但其光滑性和不可约性在一般情况下难以判定。本文的动机是为$\operatorname{Quot}^{q}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^n}^{r})$提供一组完全刻画其光滑性与不可约性的数值条件。

Method: 通过对Hilbert多项式$q$引入适当的数值条件，结合已有的Quot scheme的结构理论，分析这些条件与Quot scheme光滑性及不可约性之间的等价关系，并进一步考察lexicographic点处的局部几何性质。

Result: 给出了完全决定Quot scheme光滑性与不可约性的关于多项式$q$的数值条件，并揭示了该projective scheme的更多几何特征，包括lexicographic点的光滑性。

Conclusion: 本文给出了判定Quot scheme光滑与不可约的完整数值准则，并深化了对该方案几何结构的理解。

Abstract: The Quot scheme $\operatorname{Quot}^{q}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^n}^{r})$ parametrizes the quotients of the trivial vector bundle of rank $r$ on $n$-dimensional projective space that have Hilbert polynomial $q$ and are flat over a base scheme. We identify numerical conditions on the polynomial $q$ that completely determine when this Quot scheme is smooth and irreducible. Our approach also uncovers further geometric features of the projective scheme $\operatorname{Quot}^{q} (\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^n}^{r})$ including the smoothness of the lexicographic point.

</details>


### [134] [Effective Bialynicki-Birula-Brosnan motivic decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14485)
*Charles De Clercq*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SageMath的算法实现，用于计算各向同性约化群G的射影齐次簇X的动机分解。该算法利用Weyl群的Cayley图的深度优先搜索，将计算复杂度控制在动机大小而非整个Weyl群，并提供了例外群的新动机分解及特征2下经典群的Karpenko分解推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brosnan已证明内型各向同性约化群的射影齐次簇的动机可分解为各向异性核的射影齐次簇动机的Tate扭转直和，但该分解缺乏具体的计算方法实现。已有算法复杂度随Weyl群大小增长，对于例外群等大Weyl群情形效率低下，需要更高效的实现方案。

Method: 基于Bialynicki-Birula、Hesselink和Iversen的结果以及Brosnan的分解定理，设计了一个SageMath算法。该算法通过对Weyl群的Cayley图进行深度优先搜索（DFS）来遍历并识别分解中的各项，使得算法复杂度与动机的尺寸成正比而非与整个Weyl群的大小成正比，从而显著提高计算效率。

Result: 为某些例外群提供了新的动机分解结果，并将Karpenko关于经典群的分解结果推广到了特征2的情形。

Conclusion: 本文的SageMath实现提供了一种高效、可行的动机分解计算方法，拓展了该分解定理的适用范围，并在例外群和特征2的经典群情形产生了新的数学结果。

Abstract: Let $G$ be an isotropic reductive group and $X$ be a projective $G$-homogeneous variety. Using results from Bialynicki-Birula, Hesselink and Iversen, Brosnan showed that if $G$ is of inner type, the motive of $X$ can be expressed as a direct sum of Tate twists of motives of projective homogeneous varieties for the anisotropic kernel of $G$. We provide a SageMath implementation of this decomposition, based on a depth-first search of the Cayley graph of the Weyl group of $G$, ensuring the complexity scales with the size of the motive rather than the full Weyl group. As applications, we provide new motivic decompositions for some exceptional groups and show how to extend Karpenko's decompositions for classical groups to characteristic $2$.

</details>


### [135] [A remark on the full support property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14540)
*Yiran Cheng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对射影概形上的数值预稳定性条件，质量-Hom界蕴含全数值Grothendieck群上的支撑性质。结合近期稳定性条件的构造，对所有域上射影概形得到具有完整支撑性质的稳定性条件，并在相对情形建立统一版本，同时证明全数值稳定流形中的区分分量与极化的选择无关，从而定义典范分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支撑性质是稳定性条件理论中的核心概念，此前对任意射影概形上的稳定性条件是否具有完整支撑性质尚不明确，需要建立质量-Hom界与支撑性质之间的联系。

Method: 通过证明质量-Hom界蕴含全数值Grothendieck群上的支撑性质，结合已有的射影概形上稳定性条件的构造，并将结果推广到相对情形，最后分析稳定流形中区分分量的关于极化的不变性。

Result: 在所有域上射影概形上构造出具有完整支撑性质的稳定性条件；在相对情形下建立了统一版本的结论；证明全数值稳定流形中的区分分量不依赖于极化的选取，定义了典范分量。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了射影概形上稳定性条件理论，给出了支撑性质的充分条件，并揭示了区分分量的典范性。

Abstract: We show that a mass-Hom bound for a numerical pre-stability condition on a projective scheme over a field implies the support property with respect to the full numerical Grothendieck group. Combined with the recent construction of stability conditions on projective schemes, this yields stability conditions with full support property on every projective scheme over a field. We also establish a uniform version in the relative setting. Finally, we show that the distinguished component in the full numerical stability manifold is independent of the choice of polarization, thereby defining a canonical component.

</details>


### [136] [Compressive Domains and a Bound for the Number of Components of the Fixed Locus of a Self-Map of the Berkovich Line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14545)
*Xander Faber,Niladri Patra*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了有理函数在Berkovich射影直线上作用的"压缩域"概念，证明了该域总是包含经典不动点，并利用此事实给出有理函数不动点轨迹连通分量数目的尖锐上界。对多项式函数给出了基于Rivera-Letelier质量公式的第二种证明，最后给出了压缩域内关键权重的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非阿基米德域上有理函数在Berkovich射影直线上的动力学性质，特别是理解不动点轨迹（fixed locus）的结构。传统方法难以刻画不动点轨迹的连通分量数目，引入"压缩域"概念是为了更好地理解有理函数在Berkovich空间上的全局行为。

Method: 首先定义了有理函数作用下的"压缩域"概念，并证明压缩域必含经典不动点。利用该事实推导出不动点轨迹连通分量数目的上界。对多项式情形，采用Rivera-Letelier先前未发表的质量公式给出第二个独立的证明。最后通过分析经典不动点与边界点的数量关系，推导出压缩域内关键权重的显式公式。

Result: 证明了压缩域必然包含经典不动点；给出了有理函数不动点轨迹连通分量数目的尖锐上界；对多项式给出了基于质量公式的第二种证明；给出了压缩域内关键权重关于经典不动点数和边界点数的显式公式。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于引入压缩域概念并证明其与经典不动点之间的必然联系，从而为有理函数在Berkovich射影直线上的不动点轨迹结构提供了清晰的上界刻画，并通过多种证明路径（直接证明与质量公式证明）相互印证，最终以显式权重公式完善了压缩域理论。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of a "compressive domain" for the action of a rational function on the Berkovich projective line over a complete nontrivially-valued algebraically closed nonarchimedean field. We prove that such a domain always contains a classical fixed point, and we leverage this fact to give a sharp upper bound for the number of connected components of the fixed locus of a rational function. We give a second proof for polynomial functions that uses a previously unpublished mass formula of Rivera-Letelier. Finally, we give an explicit formula for the crucial weight inside a compressive domain as a function of the number of classical fixed points and boundary points.

</details>

### [137] [Periodic Environmental Forcing Shapes the Stability of Complex Ecological Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14081)
*Sayantan Nag Chowdhury*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对周期性环境强迫下复杂生态网络稳定性的解析理论框架。研究表明，在缓慢变化的环境中，生态系统稳定性由瞬时相互作用矩阵的时间平均最右谱边缘决定，并建立了大规模生态群落的显式稳定性判据。该理论预测了不同生态相互作用拓扑间韧性的普适层级，并通过数值模拟验证。在快速振荡条件下，高频环境波动可动态稳定原本不稳定的生态系统，揭示了静态理论所缺失的高频救援效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然生态系统的环境变异性是核心特征，但现有生态稳定性理论大多假设静态环境，无法解释真实生态系统在波动环境下的动态行为与韧性机制。

Method: 作者发展了适用于周期性环境强迫下复杂生态网络的解析稳定性理论，通过引入时间平均的瞬时相互作用矩阵最右谱边缘作为慢变环境下的稳定性判据，并分析绝热极限之外的高频振荡对系统稳定性的影响。

Result: 数值模拟验证了理论的正确性，揭示了不同生态相互作用拓扑间韧性的普适层级关系，并发现了高频振荡能够动态稳定原本不稳定生态系统的"高频救援效应"。

Conclusion: 该研究将生态稳定性理论从自治系统拓展到非自治系统，为理解波动环境中的生态系统韧性和恢复力提供了通用解析框架，填补了静态理论与真实生态动态之间的空白。

Abstract: Environmental variability is a defining feature of natural ecosystems, yet most theories of ecological stability assume static environments. Here, we develop an analytical theory of stability for complex ecological networks subjected to periodic environmental forcing. We show that, in slowly varying environments, ecosystem stability is determined by the time-averaged rightmost spectral edge of the instantaneous interaction matrix, yielding explicit stability criteria for large ecological communities. The theory predicts a universal hierarchy of resilience across ecological interaction topologies and is validated by numerical simulations. Beyond the adiabatic regime, rapid environmental oscillations dynamically stabilize otherwise unstable ecosystems, revealing a high-frequency rescue effect that is absent from static theories. These results extend ecological stability theory beyond autonomous systems and provide a general framework for understanding resilience in fluctuating environments.

</details>

### [138] [On first-order thermodynamic equilibrium conditions for fluid-fluid and solid-phase interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13603)
*Nicodemo Di Pasquale,Thomas Hudson*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 基于Larché和Cahn约束变分公式，发展了一个统一的固体-流体系统热力学框架，将体相与界面平衡条件统一为单一热力学泛函的驻点条件，涵盖体相、界面和构型热力学的共同理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期理论忽略了界面贡献的完整处理，无法描述表面效应显著的系统。因此需要发展一个将界面热力学直接纳入变分原理的统一框架，以正确处理具有重要界面效应的固体-流体系统。

Method: 从Larché和Cahn的约束变分公式出发，构建统一的固体-流体接触系统热力学框架。首先针对流体-流体系统建立底层数学结构，然后推广到固体-流体界面。通过不同类别的容许变分，使经典异质系统平衡关系自然涌现。

Result: 体相和界面平衡条件均可作为单一热力学泛函的驻点条件而获得，经典异质系统平衡关系由不同类别的容许变分自然导出，为体相、界面和构型热力学提供了统一的理论基础。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将界面热力学整合进变分原理，为处理表面效应显著的固液系统提供了统一且自洽的理论工具，弥合了体相热力学与界面热力学之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: Starting from the constrained variational formulation of Larché and Cahn for solids in contact with fluids, a unified thermodynamic framework for such systems is developed. Both bulk and interfacial equilibrium conditions arise as stationarity conditions of a single thermodynamic functional. While earlier theory neglects a full treatment of interfacial contributions and therefore cannot describe systems in which surface effects are significant, the present formulation incorporates interfacial thermodynamics directly into the variational principle. The framework is introduced first for fluid-fluid systems to establish the underlying mathematical structure, and is then extended to solid-fluid interfaces. Within this setting, classical equilibrium relations for heterogeneous systems emerge naturally from different classes of admissible variations, providing a common theoretical basis for bulk, interfacial, and configurational thermodynamics.

</details>


### [139] [Far-from-equilibrium topological phase transition in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13658)
*Ze-Min Huang,Gustav John,Sebastian Diehl*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种远离平衡态的拓扑相变机制。通过一维紧致相位模型从随机初始条件确定性演化，揭示了由拓扑与对称性而非唯象假设驱动的连续非平衡相变，其普适类被确认为有向渗流（DP），并通过有效场论与数值动力学标度分析加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索远离平衡态拓扑相变的底层机制，寻求无需唯象假设、基于拓扑与对称性的新理解途径。

Method: 构建一维紧致相位模型从随机初始条件确定性演化；利用相位紧致性产生涡旋、相位平移对称性隐含均匀不动点；建立相应的有效场论，并通过动力学标度分析进行数值验证。

Result: 揭示了涡旋诱导无序与趋向均匀弛豫之间的竞争驱动连续非平衡相变，其普适类为有向渗流（DP），数值上确认了DP临界标度行为。

Conclusion: 该机制以拓扑与对称性为核心，为远离平衡态拓扑相变提供了新的普适框架，明确其普适类为有向渗流。

Abstract: We uncover a mechanism for far-from-equilibrium topological phase transitions, via a one-dimensional compact phase model evolving deterministically from random initial conditions. It rests on topology and symmetry rather than on phenomenological postulates: phase compactness permits vortices, and a homogeneous fixed point suppresses their nucleation, with the fixed point itself implied by phase-shift symmetry. The competition between vortex-induced disordering and relaxation toward homogeneity drives a continuous nonequilibrium transition, whose universality class we identify as directed percolation (DP). We demonstrate this by constructing the corresponding effective field theory and numerically confirming DP critical scaling through dynamical-scaling analysis.

</details>


### [140] [Far-from-equilibrium scaling of non-abelian Goldstone modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13666)
*Carl Philipp Zelle,Gustav John,Orla Supple,Romain Daviet,Sebastian Diehl*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续对称性破缺与弱非平衡驱动相互作用下产生的一类非热相。作者将Kardar-Parisi-Zhang(KPZ)物理从单一SO(2)时间平移破缺推广到SO(2)×O(N)对称性的非阿贝尔时间晶体，构建了非平衡非线性sigma模型，发现旋转相中强耦合戈德斯通模具有不同的普适动力学指数，并揭示了维度依赖的非平衡扰动重正化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展KPZ普适性理论，从单一时序（时间平移破缺）推广到同时具有时间序和内部序的非阿贝尔对称性破缺情形，理解驱动量子材料、活性物质和光学诱导周期态中时间晶体与非平衡相变的基本物理规律。

Method: 构建SO(2)×O(N)对称性的非平衡非线性sigma模型，推导旋转和振荡相的戈德斯通理论，进行单圈重正化群分析，揭示KPZ-like维度结构，并通过1+1维直接数值模拟验证弱动力学标度行为。

Result: 在d=1,2维，任意弱非平衡扰动都使平衡不动点失稳并产生强耦合非热不动点；在d>2维，弱扰动无关，恢复有效平衡。旋转相中发现非传统弱动力学标度：同一序参量的不同强耦合戈德斯通扇区具有不同普适动力学指数。振荡相恢复并推广了漂移聚合物中的弱标度区。紧致性和拓扑缺陷最终破坏长程序，但留下实验可测的非热标度窗口。

Conclusion: 将KPZ普适性成功扩展到非阿贝尔对称性破缺，建立了非阿贝尔时间晶体中非平衡动力学的统一理论框架，揭示了维度相关的非平衡行为转变和新的弱动力学标度普适类，为实验观测提供了明确的理论预测。

Abstract: We identify a broad class of nonthermal phases generated by the interplay of continuous symmetry breaking and weak nonequilibrium driving. Extending Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) physics beyond the single $SO(2)$ chronon associated with periodically broken time translations, we construct nonequilibrium nonlinear sigma models for $SO(2)\times O(N)$ symmetry, describing non-Abelian time crystals with coexisting temporal and internal order. This symmetry structure arises naturally in driven quantum materials, active matter, and optically induced periodic states. For rotating and oscillating phases, we derive the Goldstone theories and show that chronon-$O(N)$ couplings remain finite deep in the ordered regime. One-loop renormalization group analysis reveals a KPZ-like dimensional structure: in $d=1,2$, arbitrarily weak nonequilibrium perturbations destabilize the equilibrium fixed point and generate strongly coupled nonthermal fixed points, realizing emergent equilibrium breaking. By contrast, for $d > 2$, weak perturbations are irrelevant and effective equilibrium is restored. A central result is unconventional weak dynamic scaling in the rotating phase: strongly coupled Goldstone sectors acquire distinct universal dynamical exponents despite belonging to the same order parameter. We characterize this scaling analytically and corroborate it through direct simulations in $1+1$ dimensions. In the oscillating phase, we recover and extend weak-scaling regimes known from drifting polymers. Finally, compactness and topological defects ultimately destroy long-range order but leave experimentally accessible nonthermal scaling windows. Together, these results extend KPZ universality to non-Abelian symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [141] [Small-world structure of quantum computer hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13855)
*S. J. da Silva Junior,D. L. Shepelyansky,J. Lages*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现量子计算机的量子寄存器态网络在残余两体位间相互作用下呈现小世界特性，最可能的Erdős数约为9，接近社会网络中Milgram报告的六度分隔。利用Åberg准则构建有效网络，发现在临界耦合强度以上该网络渗流形成覆盖几乎整个量子寄存器空间的巨连通分量，该渗流转变与量子混沌和动力学热化的起始密切相关，且可扩展到30个量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上研究量子混沌和动力学热化需要昂贵的精确对角化方法，系统规模受限。本文旨在提供一种基于网络科学的方法，利用Åberg准则构建有效网络，以更高效地分析量子多体系统中的混沌与热化现象，并大幅扩展可处理的系统尺寸。

Method: 将量子寄存器状态视为网络节点，残余两体耦合作为网络连边，构造量子态网络。采用Åberg准则过滤仅保留超过局部能级间距的相互作用以构建有效（Åberg）网络，随后分析该网络的小世界特性和渗流转变行为，并与精确对角化得到的量子混沌和热化阈值进行对比。

Result: 发现最可能的Erdős数约为9，与Milgram报告的社会网络六度分隔相当，表明量子态网络具有小世界性质。在超过临界耦合强度时，Åberg网络发生渗流转变形成覆盖几乎整个寄存器空间的巨分量。该渗流转变与精确对角化得到的量子混沌起始和动力学热化阈值高度吻合，并且该方法可将系统扩展到30个量子比特。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了量子计算中寄存器态网络与人类社会网络在拓扑结构上的相似性，并证明基于网络渗流的分析方法可以高效且准确地预测量子混沌和热化的出现，显著超越了传统精确对角化方法可达到的系统规模。

Abstract: We show that the network of quantum register states of a quantum computer (QC), coupled through residual two-body interactions between qubits, exhibits small-world properties analogous to those of complex networks found in human society. The most probable Erdős number between any two states is about 9, comparable to the six degrees of separation reported by Milgram for social networks. Using the $\mathring{A}$berg criterion, which retains only interactions exceeding the local energy spacing, we construct an effective ($\mathring{A}$berg) network and show that, above a critical coupling strength, this network percolates into a giant component spanning nearly the whole quantum register space. This percolation transition closely matches the onset of quantum chaos and dynamical thermalization established previously via costly exact diagonalization, while our approach extends the accessible system size up to $n_q=30$ qubits.

</details>


### [142] [Generalizing the multidimensional thermodynamic uncertainty relation to combinations of arbitrary counting variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14276)
*Niklas Buschmann,Udo Seifert,Alexander M. Maier*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文将多维热力学不确定关系（MTUR）推广到含时驱动过程，适用于任意初始状态、任意测量持续时间，并允许使用时间对称、时间反对称或时间不对称的计数观测变量，甚至支持粗粒化观测，从而统一了多种现有的不确定关系估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学不确定关系（TUR）为部分可观测的马尔可夫网络中隐藏量（如平均熵产生率和总动力学活动）提供下界。多维TUR（MTUR）通过利用多个观测净电流的协方差提供更紧的界，但现有推广不涵盖含时驱动过程、任意初始状态和任意测量时长，也未能统一多种类型的观测变量。

Method: 作者将MTUR推广到时间依赖驱动的马尔可夫过程，放宽了对初始状态和测量持续时间的限制；允许观测变量集合中包含时间反对称（净电流）、时间对称（交通量）和时间不对称（流动）的计数可观测量；进一步支持粗粒化观测，其中每个计数观测量可由多个底层不可区分的跃迁组成，从而在统一框架下覆盖现有多种TUR扩展。

Result: 给出了一个统一且更一般的不确定关系下界，并通过简单示例对该推广的不确定关系进行了说明和验证。

Conclusion: 该工作将MTUR、基于单电流的含时TUR扩展以及基于单一流量或交通量的估计器统一到一个一般性框架中，拓宽了热力学不确定关系在含时驱动和粗粒化观测场景下的适用性。

Abstract: Uncertainty relations provide lower bounds for otherwise hidden quantities of a partially accessible Markov network like the mean entropy production rate and the total dynamical activity. The thermodynamic uncertainty relation (TUR) is arguably the most prominent one and involves the precision of a fluctuating net current. One of its major generalizations is the multidimensional TUR (MTUR), which yields a tighter bound by using covariances of a set of observed net currents. We generalize this latter bound to time-dependently driven processes with arbitrary initial state and arbitrary measurement duration. Furthermore, this bound can be used with a set of fluctuating counting observables each of which can be time-antisymmetric, time-symmetric or time-asymmetric, i.e., a net current, a traffic or a flow. We can even allow for coarse-grained observations in which each counting observable could consist of multiple indiscernable observed transitions of the underlying system. Thus, we generalize the MTUR, extensions of the TUR for time-dependent processes based on one current, and an estimator based on one flux or on one traffic in a unifying way. We illustrate this general uncertainty relation with simple examples.

</details>


### [143] [Linear response across interaction regimes in two-dimensional ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14477)
*Aaron Müller,Pavel E. Dolgirev,Oleksii Malyshev,Eugene Demler*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于高斯函数和表示分布函数的方法，使量子玻尔兹曼方程（QBE）中的多维碰撞积分变得可处理，从而能够在二维铁磁体中精确求解线性化QBE并计算随动量和频率分辨的线性响应。该方法揭示了从低动量磁振子弱相互作用的弹道区到高动量模式强相互作用的集体流体动力学区的温度驱动转变，并成功应用于单层CrCl3，与近期氮空位中心退相干实验报道的异常磁噪声（磁振子声）结果吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维铁磁体中交换驱动的磁振子-磁振子相互作用具有强烈的动量依赖：低动量磁振子弱相互作用，高动量磁振子可强散射并表现出集体流体动力学行为。要同时描述弹道区和流体动力学区，需要能够平等捕捉这两种机制的微观描述，即量子玻尔兹曼方程（QBE）。然而QBE的求解因多维碰撞积分而极其困难，这构成了本工作的核心动机。

Method: 提出一种基于高斯函数和高效表示分布函数的方法，使碰撞积分变得可处理。该方法能够精确求解线性化QBE，并计算二维铁磁体在广泛温度和磁场范围内的动量与频率分辨的线性响应。

Result: 解析出温度驱动的交叉：从低动量弱相互作用磁振子主导的弹道区到高动量强相互作用模式主导的集体流体动力学区的转变。将方法应用于单层CrCl3，与近期氮空位中心退相干实验报道的异常磁噪声（与磁振子声一致）获得良好吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个通用框架，用于计算可在量子玻尔兹曼动力学框架内描述的在相互作用二维量子系统中的动量与频率分辨的线性响应，为理解和预测二维磁体中的自旋输运行为提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Recent discoveries of two-dimensional (2D) ferromagnets have stimulated intense interest in understanding and controlling their spin transport properties. A central microscopic feature of these systems is that exchange-driven magnon--magnon interactions are strongly momentum dependent: low-momentum magnons interact weakly, while high-momentum ones can scatter strongly and exhibit collective hydrodynamic behavior. Understanding transport in such systems therefore requires a microscopic description capable of capturing ballistic and hydrodynamic regimes on equal footing. The natural framework is the quantum Boltzmann equation (QBE), whose solution is notoriously difficult because of the multidimensional collision integrals. Here, we develop a method based on an efficient representation of distribution functions as sums of Gaussians, which renders the collision integrals tractable. This approach enables accurate solution of the linearized QBE and computation of momentum- and frequency-resolved linear response in 2D ferromagnets across a broad range of temperatures and magnetic fields. In particular, we resolve a temperature-driven crossover from a ballistic regime dominated by weakly interacting low-momentum magnons to a collective hydrodynamic regime governed by strongly interacting high-momentum modes. Applying this method to monolayer CrCl$_3$, we obtain good agreement with recent nitrogen-vacancy-center dephasing experiments that reported anomalous magnetic noise consistent with magnon sound. More broadly, our work establishes a general framework for computing momentum- and frequency-resolved linear response in interacting 2D quantum systems describable within quantum Boltzmann kinetics.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Low-dimensional Galerkin projection models for predicting turbulent secondary mean flow in a square duct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13771)
*Ahmed I. El-Nadi,Ricardo Vinuesa,Scott T. M. Dawson*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于Galerkin投影的降阶模型，通过将Navier-Stokes方程投影到线性化系统的本征模态上并添加零均值强迫，预测方形管道湍流中由侧壁引起的Prandtl第二类二次流的涌现和结构，无需事先了解湍流统计信息。最小模型仅需两个模态即可正确预测二次流的形状和方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流管道流动中侧壁的存在导致角落附近出现反向旋转的流向涡（Prandtl第二类二次流）。虽然直接数值模拟（DNS）可以捕捉这一现象，但计算成本高且对物理机制的揭示有限。因此需要开发能够预测这些湍流平均二次流涌现和结构的降阶模型，而不依赖任何先验的湍流统计知识，从而加深对二次流形成机理的理解。

Method: 将Navier-Stokes方程投影到线性化系统的本征模态上，构建降阶Galerkin模型。得到的常微分方程组通过添加零均值强迫进行模拟。使用流向常数（streamwise-constant）的本征模态构建模型，并研究不同模态数量对预测能力的影响。同时对模型系数进行解析分析，建立平均系数与其他系数联合二阶矩之间的关系。最后以相同的强迫结构运行流向常数DNS进行验证。

Result: 大多数使用领先流向常数本征模态构建的模型都能正确预测二次流的形状和方向，最小模型仅需两个模态。二次流平均贡献来源于一个具备全部二次流对称性质的本征模态的非零平均系数。该平均系数与其他模态系数联合二阶矩之间的关系可解析计算。流向常数DNS采用与降阶模型相同强迫结构时产生相似的二次流结构。模型预测的Reynolds应力分布与完全解析DNS定性一致，且模型维度增加时一致性提高。

Conclusion: 降阶Galerkin模型能够以极低的维度（最少两个模态）捕捉方形管道湍流中Prandtl第二类二次流的涌现与结构，揭示了二次流平均贡献与本征模态系数二阶统计量之间的解析关系，为理解湍流平均流动的形成机制提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The presence of sidewalls in turbulent duct flows leads to the emergence of secondary flow structures in the form of counter-rotating streamwise vortices near the corners (Prandtl's secondary flow of the second kind). This work develops reduced-order Galerkin projection models that can predict the emergence and structure of these turbulent mean secondary flows in a square duct geometry, without requiring any prior knowledge of the turbulent statistics. The models are obtained by projecting the Navier--Stokes equations onto eigenmodes of the linearised system, with the resulting systems of ordinary differential equations simulated with the addition of zero-mean forcing. We show that most models obtained using leading streamwise-constant eigenmodes predict the correct shape and direction of the secondary mean, with the minimal such model requiring only two modes. In these models, the secondary mean contribution arises due to the nonzero average coefficient of an eigenmode that possesses all of the symmetry properties expected of a secondary mean. The models are sufficiently simple such that the relationship between this mean coefficient and the joint second moment of other mode coefficients can be computed analytically. We confirm that running streamwise-constant direct numerical simulations (DNS) with the same forcing structure as used in the reduced-order models produces similar secondary-flow structures. We additionally demonstrate that our models produce qualitatively similar Reynolds stress distributions to fully resolved direct numerical simulations, with improved agreement as model dimension increases.

</details>


### [145] [Cross-frequency amplification of perturbations in a laminar separation bubble using resolvent analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13788)
*Md Rashidul Islam,Yiyang Sun*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过大涡模拟(LES)分析平板逆压梯度引起的层流分离泡(LSB)，在无外部激励下自维持涡脱落。结合谱本征正交分解(SPOD)与经典/谐波可解性分析，发现驻定展向条纹并非由同频线性放大维持，而是通过基频非定常性将斜向开尔文-亥姆霍兹波与驻定条纹耦联的跨频放大机制所驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层流分离泡中观察到的涡脱落和近零频驻定条纹是重要的流动现象，但经典可解性分析无法解释条纹的能量来源——它预测低频区域增益极小，与SPOD观测到的显著条纹能量相矛盾。作者旨在揭示这一差异背后的物理机制，即条纹并非通过同频线性放大获得能量。

Method: 在基于位移厚度的雷诺数为410、自由来流马赫数0.25条件下进行LES模拟。对LES数据施加SPOD识别主导相干结构（二维/斜向KH波和驻定周期条纹）。对平均流做经典可解性分析（resolvent analysis），再对时间周期基流做谐波可解性分析（harmonic resolvent analysis），比较两种框架下的增益。

Result: SPOD识别出涡脱落频率处的2D和斜向KH波以及近零频的驻定展向条纹。经典分析预测KH波存在强对流放大但条纹区域增益小且无主导秩一机制；谐波分析表明基流非定常性将斜向KH波跨频耦联至驻定条纹，增益远大于直接同频放大。

Conclusion: 驻定条纹并非通过同频线性放大维持，而是由基流的非定常性驱动的跨频放大机制从涡脱落频率的KH波获取能量。这一跨频路径为LSB中近再附着区驻定条纹的能量来源提供了合理解释，也说明了经典可解性框架在处理此类问题时需扩展至谐波/跨频视角。

Abstract: A large-eddy simulation (LES) of a laminar separation bubble (LSB) induced by an adverse pressure gradient over a flat plate is performed at an inflow displacement-thickness-based Reynolds number of 410 and a free-stream Mach number of 0.25. With a mean peak reverse flow of 21.4%, the bubble sustains self-excited vortex shedding through a local region of absolute instability, in the absence of any external forcing. Spectral proper orthogonal decomposition (SPOD) applied to the LES data identifies three dominant coherent structures within the LSB: two-dimensional and oblique Kelvin--Helmholtz (KH) waves in the separated shear layer at the vortex-shedding frequency, and stationary spanwise-periodic streaks near reattachment at near-zero frequency. Classical resolvent analysis of the mean flow identifies strong convective amplification of the KH waves over a range of spanwise wavenumbers, but predicts only weak amplification in the low-frequency, streak-forming region, where the leading gain is orders of magnitude smaller and no dominant rank-one mechanism is present. This discrepancy with the SPOD energy indicates that the streaks are not sustained by same-frequency linear amplification, but are instead energized by the intrinsic forcing, which the classical framework treats as an unexplained input. Harmonic resolvent analysis of the time-periodic base flow reveals the underlying mechanism: the base-flow unsteadiness couples the oblique KH wave at the shedding frequency to the stationary streak through cross-frequency amplification, yielding a gain far larger than that of the direct same-frequency amplification. This cross-frequency route provides a likely explanation for how the stationary streaks observed near reattachment are energized.

</details>


### [146] [Elastic wakes mediate collective viscoelastic fluid-structure interactions in side-by-side cantilever arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13908)
*Arisa Yokokoji,Amy Q. Shen,Simon J. Haward*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过系统对比弱剪切变稀（WS）和强剪切变稀（HS）的聚氧化乙烯溶液在并排柔性悬臂阵列中的流固耦合行为，揭示了弹性尾流合并、流动发散和悬臂向内偏转等集体不稳定性依赖于流体弹性和剪切变稀特性及阵列几何构型的共同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低雷诺数下粘弹性流体流经可变形结构的流固耦合（FSI）现象虽与纤毛、鞭毛等生物系统及工程微系统密切相关，但目前对其理解仍十分有限，缺乏系统性的机理认识。

Method: 采用自下而上的方法，系统变化悬臂数量（从单根到三根）和测试流体的流变特性（弱剪切变稀WS与强剪切变稀HS的聚氧化乙烯溶液），通过实验比较不同Weissenberg数（Wi）下的尾流形态、流场和悬臂偏转行为。

Result: 两种流体均随Wi增大形成拉伸弹性尾流；WS流体在临界Wi*处发生从分离尾流到合并尾流的转变，伴随发散流场和悬臂向内协调偏转，且Wi*随悬臂数量从二增至三而增大；HS流体在整个Wi范围内均未出现尾流合并、流动发散和向内偏转，尽管其具有相当的弹性和弹性尾流。

Conclusion: 弹性本身足以产生尾流但不足以引发集体不稳定性，集体不稳定性需要剪切变稀效应和相邻悬臂间相互作用的共同参与。粘弹性FSI由流体弹性、剪切变稀和几何构型三者的组合效应共同支配，弹性尾流相互作用是连接局部尾流与阵列尺度结构响应的关键机制。

Abstract: Fluid-structure interaction (FSI) in viscoelastic flows past deformable structures at low Reynolds numbers remains poorly understood, despite its relevance to biological systems such as cilia and flagella, and to engineered microsystems. We investigate viscoelastic FSI in side-by-side flexible cantilever arrays using a bottom-up approach that systematically varies the number of cantilevers and the rheology of the test fluid, comparing weakly shear-thinning (WS) and highly shear-thinning (HS) polyethylene oxide solutions. For both fluids, with increasing Weissenberg number (Wi), an elongated elastic wake develops behind a single isolated cantilever. For multiple cantilevers, the WS fluid undergoes a transition at a critical Weissenberg number (Wi*) from separated to merged elastic wakes, accompanied by the emergence of a divergent flow field and coordinated inward spanwise cantilever deflection. The critical Wi* increases as the number of cantilevers in the array is increased from two to three, demonstrating a strong dependence of the onset on the array configuration. For the HS fluid, wake merger, flow divergence, and inward cantilever deflection are suppressed across the full range of Wi investigated, despite comparable elasticity and the formation of elastic wakes. This contrast shows that elasticity alone promotes wake formation but is insufficient to produce the collective instability, which instead requires both shear-thinning and interactions between neighboring cantilevers. These findings demonstrate that viscoelastic FSI in flexible arrays is governed by the combined effects of fluid elasticity, shear-thinning, and geometric configuration, with elastic wake interactions mediating the collective instability and linking local elastic wakes to array-scale structural response.

</details>


### [147] [Long-time behavior of optimal mixing in an advection-diffusion shell model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14346)
*Jiajia Guo,Baole Wen,Christian Seis,Charles R. Doering*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文在壳模型框架下研究平流-扩散方程中最优混合的长时间行为，量化了在涡度约束搅拌下H^-1混合范数的衰减。数值结果表明扩散存在时标量长度尺度受广义Batchelor尺度限制，H^-1范数呈指数衰减且衰减率独立于扩散系数κ；与纯平流情形相比，扩散显著增强了长时间混合速率。基于数值观察推导了条件性下界和上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解最优混合在长时间尺度上的行为是湍流混合理论的核心问题之一。特别是扩散与平流共同作用下的混合效率、以及混合率对扩散系数的依赖性，此前缺乏精确的定量理解和严格的理论保证。

Method: 采用壳模型框架进行数值模拟，对比局部时间最优(最大化瞬时混合率)与全局时间最优(在指定最终时刻最大化混合度)两种优化策略，对κ>0和κ=0两种情形进行长时间计算，并结合理论分析推导条件性下界与上界。

Result: 数值结果揭示了广义Batchelor尺度对混合的限制；H^-1混合范数在扩散存在时呈指数衰减，衰减率与扩散系数κ无关；扩散增强了长时间混合率，且增大κ通过减小指数衰减的前置因子进一步改善混合效率。理论上建立了扩散系数无关的指数衰减下界，以及增强耗散最大速率的上界(有效扩散时间尺度至少为|logκ|阶)。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了最优混合长时间行为的数值与理论一致性认识：扩散不仅在本质上改变混合的衰减机制(从代数到指数)，还使衰减率对扩散系数具有鲁棒性(不敏感性)。理论边界与数值结果吻合，为理解平流-扩散耦合系统中标量混合的极限效率提供了重要指导。

Abstract: We investigate the long-time behavior of optimal mixing in an advection-diffusion equation using a shell model framework. Our focus is on quantifying the decay of the scalar variance, measured by the negative Sobolev norm $H^{-1}$, under enstrophy-constrained stirring. We perform long-time computations using both local-in-time (maximizing the instantaneous mixing rate) and global-in-time (maximizing mixedness at a prescribed final time) optimization strategies. For mixing with diffusion ($κ>0$), the numerical results show that the scalar length scale eventually becomes limited by a generalized Batchelor scale, in close agreement with theoretical predictions. In this regime, the $H^{-1}$ mix-norm decays exponentially in time with a decay rate that is independent of the diffusivity $κ$. Compared with the purely advective case ($κ= 0$), diffusion significantly enhances the long-time mixing rate; moreover, increasing diffusivity further improves mixing efficiency by reducing the prefactor of the exponential decay. Guided by these numerical observations, we derive new conditional lower bounds on the $H^{-1}$ norm whose exponential decay rates are strictly independent of the diffusivity parameter $κ$, for all $κ> 0$. We further establish conditional upper bounds on the maximal rate of enhanced dissipation of the scalar variance, showing that the effective diffusion time scale is at least of the order $|\logκ|$.

</details>


### [148] [Data-driven modeling of hypersonic flows in chemical non-equilibrium with catalytic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14445)
*Konstantinos Sarras,Louis Walpot,Thierry Magin,Peter Schmid,Taraneh Sayadi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文首次将Scherding等人(2023)提出的数据驱动降阶框架应用于具有局部催化表面不连续性的高超声速反应流，通过引入Sammon型应力惩罚增强非线性降维方法，缓解潜在流形的拓扑折叠问题，在保持精度的同时将模拟成本降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高超声速流动中极端热化学非平衡导致化学-辐射-能量交换强耦合，催化壁面反应显著影响边界层组分和表面传热。高保真反应流模拟需重复评估详细热化学库，计算成本高昂，成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 基于Scherding等人(2023)的数据驱动降阶框架，结合非线性降维、社区聚类和局部替代模型；在此基础上对降维方法增加Sammon型应力惩罚以缓解特征流形拓扑折叠，提升聚类和替代模型的鲁棒性，从而处理催化表面不连续性带来的复杂热化学状态空间。

Result: 模型能准确捕捉不连续催化性质的影响，包括壁面物种质量分数、扩散通量和表面传热的尖锐梯度，同时将总体模拟成本降低50%且不牺牲精度。

Conclusion: Sammon型应力惩罚增强的降阶框架能有效处理高超声速反应流中催化表面不连续性带来的复杂状态空间，实现显著的计算加速而保持精度，为高保真反应流模拟提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Hypersonic flows involve extreme thermochemical non-equilibrium, where strong energy dissipation leads to tightly coupled chemical reactions, radiation, and energy exchange. In this regime, surface chemistry, particularly catalytic wall reactions, can significantly affect boundary-layer composition and surface heat transfer. Accurate simulations of such flows may require repeated evaluations of detailed thermochemical libraries, which represent a major computational bottleneck in high-fidelity reactive-flow simulations. To mitigate this cost, we employ the data-driven reduced-order framework introduced by Scherding et al. (2023), which combines nonlinear dimensionality reduction, community clustering, and local surrogate models to efficiently approximate high-dimensional thermochemical mappings. In this work, this framework is extended for the first time to hypersonic reactive flows with localized catalytic surface discontinuities, introducing sharp variations in wall chemistry and heat transfer. To address the increased complexity of the thermochemical state space, the dimensionality reduction method is enhanced with a Sammon-type stress penalty that mitigates topological folding of the latent manifold and improves the robustness of the clustering and surrogate stages. The resulting model accurately captures the effects of discontinuous catalytic properties, including sharp gradients in wall species mass fractions, diffusion fluxes, and surface heat transfer, while reducing the overall simulation cost by 50% without compromising accuracy.

</details>

### [149] [Learning Unsteady Aneurysm Hemodynamics with Physics-Informed DeepONets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13629)
*Oscar L. Cruz-Gonzalez,Valérie Deplano,Badih Ghattas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出M3PI-DeepONet架构，用于在理想化腹主动脉瘤（AAA）几何中预测非定常三维血流的速度和压力场。该模型采用聚合注入（Aggregated Injection）策略融合多输入分支的潜在表示，并结合逐层门控机制与多分支算子网络拓扑，以3D Navier-Stokes方程作为物理约束（仅需0.3%标注内部数据）进行训练，实现平均相对L2速度误差低于4%、压力误差约5%，推理速度较参考CFD模拟提升约36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床可操作的患者特异性血流动力学评估（如壁面剪应力、涡结构和压力分布）对判断腹主动脉瘤的危险演化至关重要。虽然PI-DeepONets在补充CFD方面有前景，但面对复杂三维流动仍存在架构性挑战，亟需设计更适配的深度学习架构来高效预测非定常三维血流场。

Method: 提出Modified Multi-Input Multi-Output PI-DeepONets（M3PI-DeepONet）：(1) 采用聚合注入策略，先将多个输入分支的潜在表示融合再进行主干网络注入，使坐标基适应多个物理约束；(2) 首次将逐层门控机制与多分支算子网络拓扑结合，构建输入自适应的主干基；(3) 集成3D Navier-Stokes方程作为物理定律，基于物理信息残差、初始条件和边界条件联合训练，仅使用0.3%标注内部数据及选定的分支条件信号。

Result: 该模型能同时预测非定常3D血流速度和压力场，平均相对L2速度误差低于4%，压力误差约5%；在分支输入条件可用时，相较参考CFD模拟实现约36倍的保守循环推理加速。

Conclusion: 这项工作推进了深度学习在心血管疾病建模中的应用，标志着向实时、无创临床诊断迈出重要一步，为AAA的血流动力学快速评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Clinically actionable, patient-specific hemodynamic assessment, specifically wall shear stress, vortex structure and pressure distributions, is critical for determining risky or unfavorable evolution in Abdominal Aortic Aneurysms (AAA). While Physics-Informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) show promising results in complementing established 5 tools such as Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD), a persistent architectural challenge remains for complex 3D flows. In this direction, we propose a Modified Multi-Input Multi-Output PI-DeepONets (M3PI-DeepONet) designed for predicting unsteady flows in an idealized AAA geometry. Central to our model is the Aggregated Injection strategy, where latent representations from multiple input branches are fused prior to trunk injection, allowing the coordinate basis to adapt to multiple physical constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first architecture to combine the layer-wise gating mechanism with a multi-branch operator-network topology, yielding an input-adaptive trunk basis. Additionally, we integrate the 3D Navier-Stokes equations as governing physical laws, so the model is trained based on physics-informed residuals, initial and boundary conditions, and only 0.3% of the labeled internal data together with the selected branch-conditioning signals. The M3PI-DeepONet simultaneously predicts unsteady 3D flow velocity and pressure fields with an average relative L2 velocity error below 4% and pressure error around 5% while achieving a conservative retained-cycle inference speedup of approximately 36x compared to reference CFD simulations once the branch inputs used for conditioning are available. This work advances the application of deep learning in cardiovascular disease modeling, marking step toward real-time, non-invasive clinical diagnostics.

</details>


### [150] [On the Brittleness of Maximum Likelihood Estimation for Gaussian Process Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13793)
*Tyler R. Johnson,Kian Ben-Jacob,Christopher P. Muller,Ramin Bostanabad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统地评估了最大似然估计（MLE）在训练高斯过程（GP）用于概率回归和分类任务时的脆弱性，提出了理论上有依据的替代指标和实用解决方案，并在贝叶斯优化等下游任务中验证了其有效性，所训练的GP甚至能在预测精度、不确定性量化和推理成本方面超越表格基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLE是训练ML模型最常用的方法，但其在假设不满足时会导致模型泛化能力差。高斯过程虽然被广泛认为对过拟合具有鲁棒性，但实际上同样受MLE脆弱性的影响。作者希望从根本上评估这一脆弱性，并为工程设计中构建更可靠的GP提供指导。

Method: 作者将理论上有根据的度量指标与MLE进行对比，系统性地评估MLE在GP训练（概率回归和分类）中的脆弱性，提出实用替代方案，并通过广泛实验验证其在下游工程设计任务（如贝叶斯优化）中的有效性。

Result: 实验表明所提出的解决方案在贝叶斯优化等下游设计任务中表现出色，构建的GP在预测精度、不确定性量化和推理成本方面甚至优于表格基础模型（如TabPFN），相关代码已在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: MLE在GP训练中确实存在脆性问题，但通过采用理论上更合理的替代指标和实用解决方案，可以构建准确且稳健的GP模型，为工程实践者提供了构建可靠概率模型的蓝图。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) has become an indispensable part of modern engineering design workflows. A crucial step in training an ML model is the selection of the loss function which can be systematically formulated via various techniques such as maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and cross-validation . While MLE is one of the most popular, effective, and intuitive mechanisms for training ML models, it is brittle: if the assumptions underpinning it are not met, the trained ML model may generalize poorly. This brittleness affects even Gaussian processes (GPs) which are widely used in engineering design and are often (incorrectly) presumed to be very robust to overfitting. In this paper, we fundamentally evaluate the brittleness of MLE in the context of training GPs for probabilistic regression or classification tasks. We compare theoretically grounded metrics against MLE and propose practical solutions. Our extensive studies demonstrate the effectiveness of our solutions in downstream design tasks such as Bayesian optimization and provide a blueprint for practitioners to build accurate and robust GPs that can even outperform tabular foundation models in terms of prediction accuracy, uncertainty quantification, and inference cost. Our contributions are publicly available via GitHub at https://github.com/Bostanabad-Research-Group/GP-vs-TabPFN-vs-GPyTorch.

</details>


### [151] [Deep Vision in Smart Manufacturing: MODERN Framework for Intelligent Quality Monitoring and Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13937)
*Yicheng Kang,Yuling Jiao,Xin Geng,Mahesh Nagarajan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出MODERN框架，一个基于深度学习的工业质量监控与故障隔离系统，利用inception残差神经网络构建控制图监控缺陷概率，并通过迁移学习识别缺陷区域。理论上建立了缺陷似然估计和故障诊断的极小极大最优收敛率，实证上在模拟和真实数据上均优于现有方法，且得出一个反直觉的管理启示：制造商不一定总值得升级监控设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造生产系统配备大量传感器和成像设备，这些设备虽能促进通信和智能管理，但如何将这些增强能力有效整合到工业质量控制实践中，实现高质量的缺陷监控与故障隔离，仍是一个挑战。现有方法在处理训练数据不足、缺陷区域识别以及理论最优性保证方面存在局限。

Method: 1) 采用inception残差神经网络架构构建控制图，监控产品含缺陷的似然概率；2) 提出基于迁移学习的故障区域估计器，用于识别缺陷区域；3) 提出只需小样本的迁移监控技术处理训练数据不足的情况；4) 提出假设检验方法定量评估所提方法的适用性；5) 从理论上推导缺陷似然估计和故障诊断的极小极大最优收敛率。

Result: 理论上建立了缺陷似然估计和故障诊断的极小极大最优收敛率；实证上在模拟实验和真实数据上均展示出优于现有最先进方法的表现。此外，研究得出一个反直觉的管理启示：制造商不一定总是应该不计成本地升级其监控设备。

Conclusion: MODERN框架为智能制造中的质量监控和故障隔离提供了有效的深度学习方法，兼具理论最优性和实际优越性。研究还揭示了监控设备升级决策的复杂性，提示制造商应在设备升级成本与质量控制收益之间进行更审慎的权衡。

Abstract: Smart manufacturing processes are often installed with a large number of sensors, imaging devices and computers, which not only enable instant communication across various modules of a production system but also aid in intelligent manufacturing management. In this paper, we introduce MODERN, a deep learning framework for quality monitoring and fault isolation, which integrates these enhanced capabilities into the practice of industrial quality control. Using the architecture of an inception residual neural network, we develop a control chart that monitors the likelihood of a product containing defects. We also propose a faulty region estimator that identifies the defective area using transfer learning. To extend our framework to cases where there are not sufficient training data, we suggest a transfer monitoring technique that requires only a small sample size and a hypothesis testing approach for quantitatively assessing the applicability of our method. Theoretically, we establish the minimax optimal convergence rate for both our defect likelihood estimation and fault diagnosis. Our results lead to a seemingly counter-intuitive managerial implication - it may not always be in a manufacturer's best interests to keep upgrading its monitoring equipment regardless of the cost. Empirically, we demonstrate the superior performance of our method in comparison with a state-of-the-art approach using both simulated experiments and real data.

</details>


### [152] [Extending Occam's inversion with lasso fusion, overcomplete dictionaries, and isotropic total variation regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14225)
*Anandaroop Ray*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了Occam反演框架下的l1正则化地球物理反演问题，阐明了1D与多维情况下的数学统一性，并对比了坐标下降、IRLS和分裂Bregman三种求解器在不同维度问题中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管l2正则化的Occam反演被广泛用于获得平滑模型，l1正则化可以提供视觉上尖锐的模型，但地球物理学中的l1正则化反演与统计学和成像领域的文献脱节，对多维总变分（TV）的两种形式及其方向不变性常常被忽视，缺乏统一且清晰的数学框架。

Method: 在Occam框架内，针对l2数据范数与l1模型正则化，证明一维情况下合成（synthesis）与分析（analysis）两种路径得到相同的fused lasso问题；将框架推广到多维并纳入字典类算子（如小波变换）；解析了坐标下降、迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）和分裂Bregman三种求解器背后的数学原理，并通过线性（1D回归、2D去模糊）和非线性（1D航空瞬变电磁）系列问题及野外数据进行验证。

Result: 1D问题推荐坐标下降；2D问题推荐IRLS而非分裂Bregman，且IRLS所需的最小二乘求解次数最多可减少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过将l1正则化反演的各种形式呈现为熟悉的Occam反演框架，本文有望促进地球物理学家更广泛地采纳l1正则化反演方法。

Abstract: Occam's inversion is a robust algorithm to perform nonlinear geophysical inversion. It provides the smoothest model within observation noise, thereby discouraging geological overinterpretation. While Occam originally penalised l2 model roughness, l1 can be used to provide models that are visually sharp. However, l1 regularised geophysical inversion has diverged from the larger body of statistics and imaging literature. For example, l1 regularisation with a difference operator (i.e., total variation) in multiple dimensions has two distinct forms, only one of which is invariant to edge orientation -- a distinction often overlooked in geophysics. In one dimension this reduces to the fused lasso problem in statistics. Within the Occam framework, for l2 data norm and l1 model regularisation, we show that lasso fusion through either synthesis or analysis leads to the same 1D problem. We extend the framework to multiple dimensions and to operators such as wavelet transforms in a dictionary, unravelling the mathematics behind three commonly used solvers for l1 regularised problems. These are coordinate descent, iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS), and split Bregman. We use them to solve a sequence of problems that are linear (1D regression, 2D deblurring) and nonlinear (1D airborne transient electromagnetics) including a field data example. We recommend coordinate descent for 1D problems, and IRLS over split Bregman for 2D problems, with IRLS requiring up to an order of magnitude fewer least squares solves. We hope this work will further encourage geophysicists to adopt l1 regularised inversion, by presenting its various forms as a familiar Occam's inversion.

</details>


### [153] [Offline Deep Q* Estimation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14401)
*Xiaohong Chen,Yuling Jiao,Lican Kang,Jerry Zhijian Yang,Chen Zhong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的离线强化学习框架，将最优Bellman算子的估计与价值函数学习解耦：先用条件扩散模型估计奖励分布和转移核，再将这些估计器代入Bellman方程，通过最小化经验Bellman残差在神经网络函数类上学习Q*的深度估计器。理论上建立了最优Bellman算子学习的非渐近收敛率（基于总变差距离的端到端条件扩散分析）、残差风险的oracle最优率，以及在可集中性条件下的L²收敛率，且不依赖深度RL理论中常见的完备性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RL中估计最优动作价值函数Q*可归结为基于离线观测求解最优Bellman方程，但奖励函数与转移核未知，导致最优Bellman算子无法直接从数据观测到。现有方法面临这一根本性挑战，需要一种能有效估计Bellman算子并保证理论收敛性的新框架。

Method: 将算子估计与价值函数学习解耦为两步：（1）利用条件扩散模型估计奖励律和转移核，由此构造数据驱动的近似最优Bellman算子；（2）将这些估计器代入Bellman方程，在神经网络函数类上最小化经验Bellman残差来获得Q*的深度估计器。理论上采用端到端的条件扩散估计分析（在总变差距离下），并结合Hölder光滑性和可集中性条件推导各阶段收敛率。

Result: 建立了最优Bellman算子学习的非渐近收敛率；得到超额Bellman残差风险的oracle阶段性最优率Õ(n^{-2β/(d_x+d_a+2β)})；在可集中性条件下，将残差界转化为Q*深度估计器的L²收敛率Õ(n^{-β/(d_x+d_a+2β)})，其中d_x和d_a分别为状态和动作空间维度，β为Q*的Hölder光滑性指数。广泛的数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的解耦框架在理论和实证两方面均表现优异：其理论分析不依赖深度RL常用的完备性假设，具有更广泛的适用性；所得到的收敛率达到了oracle阶段性最优水平，并通过数值实验证实了该方法的强实证性能。

Abstract: In offline RL, estimating the optimal action-value function $Q^*$ can be formulated as solving the optimal Bellman equation based solely on offline observations. A fundamental challenge is that the reward function and transition kernel are unknown, so the optimal Bellman operator is not directly observable from data. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that decouples operator estimation from value function learning. In this approach, we first formulate conditional diffusion models to estimate the reward law and transition kernel, which induces a data-driven approximation of the optimal Bellman operator. We then plug these estimators into the Bellman equation and obtain a deep estimator of $Q^*$ by minimizing the empirical Bellman residual over a neural network function class. Theoretically, we first establish sharp nonasymptotic convergence rates for learning the optimal Bellman operator through an end-to-end analysis of conditional diffusion estimation in total variation distance. We then establish the oracle value-stage rate $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(n^{-\frac{2β}{d_x+d_a+2β}}\bigr)$ for the excess Bellman residual risk. Finally, under a concentrability condition, we translate this residual bound into an $L^2$ convergence rate of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(n^{-\fracβ{d_x+d_a+2β}}\bigr)$ for the resulting deep estimator of $Q^*$, where $d_x$ and $d_a$ denote the dimensions of the state and action spaces, respectively, and $β$ denotes the Hölder smoothness index of $Q^*$. Importantly, our theoretical analysis does not rely on completeness assumptions commonly used in deep RL theory. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method and its strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [154] [Online Inference in Distributional Temporal-Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14408)
*Yang Peng,Liangyu Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定策略下，基于单条马尔可夫轨迹对回报分布泛函进行在线统计推断的问题。作者使用非参数分布时序差分学习估计回报分布，证明了Polyak-Ruppert平均估计量的根号T误差弱收敛到Cramér空间中的中心高斯随机元，并建立了相应的bootstrap一致性理论，为光滑和非光滑统计泛函（如方差、CVaR、期望缺口、expectile及分位数）的推断提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的回报分布函数（如方差、CVaR、分位数等）在很多决策场景中比期望回报更为重要，但在线统计推断（即从单条轨迹中估计并推断这些分布泛函）在理论上缺乏系统性保证。现有工作主要聚焦于期望回报的推断，而对分布层面泛函的推断尤其是bootstrap方法的有效性缺乏严格的理论分析。

Method: 本文采用非参数分布时序差分学习（distributional temporal-difference learning）对固定策略下的回报分布进行估计，然后针对Polyak-Ruppert平均估计量建立中心极限定理。对于光滑统计泛函，证明根号T误差弱收敛到Cramér空间中的高斯随机元，并证明条件bootstrap的一致性。对于非光滑统计泛函（如分位数），开发了在T^{-1/2}邻域内的局部渐近理论，并给出对应的bootstrap类比结果。

Result: 证明了Polyak-Ruppert平均估计量的根号T误差在Cramér空间中弱收敛到中心高斯随机元；条件bootstrap估计与原估计的根号T差异弱收敛到相同的高斯极限。此结果支持方差、CVaR、期望缺口和expectile等光滑泛函的bootstrap推断。对于非光滑泛函，建立了回报CDF在有限阈值附近T^{-1/2}邻域的局部渐近理论及其bootstrap类比，支持回报分位数等由CDF方程刻画的非光滑泛函的推断。

Conclusion: 该工作为强化学习中回报分布泛函的在线统计推断提供了统一的理论框架，填补了分布层面bootstrap推断的空缺，覆盖了从光滑泛函到非光滑泛函（如分位数）的完整谱系，对实际应用中回报风险评估与推断具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We study online statistical inference for functionals of the return distribution under a fixed policy. The return distribution is estimated by nonparametric distributional temporal-difference learning from a single Markov trajectory. For the Polyak--Ruppert averaged estimator, we prove that its root-$T$ error converges weakly to a centered Gaussian random element in Cramér space. We also prove that, conditionally on the observed trajectory, the root-$T$ difference between the bootstrap and original averages converges weakly to the same Gaussian limit. These results justify bootstrap inference for smooth statistical functionals, including variance, CVaR, expected shortfall, and expectiles. For nonsmooth statistical functionals, we develop a local asymptotic theory for the estimated return CDF over $T^{-1/2}$-neighborhoods of finitely many thresholds, together with its bootstrap analogue. This theory allows us to conduct inference for nonsmooth statistical functionals characterized by CDF equations, including return quantiles.

</details>
